File: scripts/analyze_deep_results.js

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const results = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'deep_check_results.json'), 'utf8'));

// Filter out noise and list really risky patterns
const genuineRisks = results.filter(r => {
  // Check if it's new Date(xxx).toISOString() or .getTime()
  if (r.type === 'Unsafe new Date method call') return true;
  if (r.type === 'Unsafe array index property access') return true;
  // If property access on data/record/client
  return true;
});

console.log(`Analyzing ${genuineRisks.length} candidates...`);

// Group by file
const byFile = {};
genuineRisks.forEach(r => {
  if (!byFile[r.file]) byFile[r.file] = [];
  byFile[r.file].push(r);
});

Object.keys(byFile).forEach(file => {
  console.log(`\n=== File: ${file} (${byFile[file].length} issues) ===`);
  byFile[file].forEach(issue => {
    console.log(`  [Line ${issue.line}] ${issue.type}: ${issue.code}`);
    console.log(`    Code: ${issue.fullLine}`);
  });
});


================================================================================
File: scripts/audit_runtime_errors.js
================================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function getTsxFiles(dir) {
  let results = [];
  const list = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  list.forEach(file => {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, file);
    const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    if (stat && stat.isDirectory()) {
      results = results.concat(getTsxFiles(fullPath));
    } else if (file.endsWith('.tsx')) {
      results.push(fullPath);
    }
  });
  return results;
}

const files = getTsxFiles(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'app'));

const findings = [];

files.forEach(file => {
  const relPath = path.relative(path.join(__dirname, '..'), file);
  const content = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  const lines = content.split('\n');

  lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
    const lineNum = idx + 1;

    // Check 1: Unsafe .toISOString() on new Date() or variable
    if (line.includes('.toISOString()')) {
      // If called directly on something that might be Invalid Date
      if (/new Date\([^)]+\)\.toISOString\(\)/.test(line)) {
        // e.g. new Date(item.date).toISOString() without null check
        findings.push({
          type: 'Unsafe toISOString',
          severity: 'HIGH',
          file: relPath,
          line: lineNum,
          code: line.trim(),
          desc: 'new Date(...) に渡す値が null/undefined または不正な場合、toISOString() で RangeError: Invalid time value クラッシュが発生するリスク'
        });
      }
    }

    // Check 2: Direct .getTime() on nullable Date
    if (line.includes('.getTime()')) {
      if (/new Date\([^)]+\)\.getTime\(\)/.test(line)) {
        findings.push({
          type: 'Potential NaN in getTime',
          severity: 'MEDIUM',
          file: relPath,
          line: lineNum,
          code: line.trim(),
          desc: 'new Date(...) の引数が undefined の場合、getTime() が NaN になり数値比較や計算が破綻するリスク'
        });
      }
    }

    // Check 3: Unsafe .map( without optional chaining on prop/state
    const mapMatch = line.match(/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\.map\(/);
    if (mapMatch && !line.includes('?.map(')) {
      const varName = mapMatch[1];
      // ignore Array, Object, common static arrays
      const safeVars = ['DAYS', 'Array', 'months', 'weeks', 'headers', 'tabs', 'columns'];
      if (!safeVars.includes(varName) && !line.includes(`${varName} &&`) && !line.includes(`(${varName} ||`)) {
        // Check if varName is preceded by something nullable
        if (line.includes(`data.${varName}`) || line.includes(`client.${varName}`) || line.includes(`props.${varName}`)) {
          findings.push({
            type: 'Unsafe .map() call',
            severity: 'HIGH',
            file: relPath,
            line: lineNum,
            code: line.trim(),
            desc: `${varName} が null または undefined の場合、TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'map') で画面全体がクラッシュ`
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // Check 4: JSON.parse without try-catch
    if (line.includes('JSON.parse(') && !line.includes('try') && !lines.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 5), idx).some(l => l.includes('try {'))) {
      findings.push({
        type: 'Unprotected JSON.parse',
        severity: 'MEDIUM',
        file: relPath,
        line: lineNum,
        code: line.trim(),
        desc: 'JSON.parse が try-catch で保護されておらず、不正な JSON 文字列や null で SyntaxError クラッシュするリスク'
      });
    }

    // Check 5: Hydration error: direct toLocaleDateString() or toLocaleTimeString() in JSX
    if (/toLocale(Date|Time|String)/.test(line) && (line.includes('{') || line.includes('<'))) {
      findings.push({
        type: 'Hydration mismatch risk',
        severity: 'LOW',
        file: relPath,
        line: lineNum,
        code: line.trim(),
        desc: 'toLocaleDateString / toLocaleTimeString を JSX で直接評価すると、サーバーとクライアントのロケール・タイムゾーン差で Hydration Error が発生するリスク'
      });
    }
  });
});

console.log(`Scan completed. Found ${findings.length} potential issues across ${files.length} files.`);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'audit_results.json'), JSON.stringify(findings, null, 2));
console.log('Results written to scripts/audit_results.json');


================================================================================
File: scripts/check-fks.ts
================================================================================

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

function checkForeignKeys(dbPath: string, targetTable: string) {
  const db = new Database(dbPath);
  const tables = db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all() as Array<{ name: string }>;
  console.log(`\n==================================================`);
  console.log(`Tables referencing [${targetTable}] in ${dbPath}`);
  console.log(`==================================================`);
  
  for (const t of tables) {
    try {
      const fks = db.prepare(`PRAGMA foreign_key_list("${t.name}")`).all() as any[];
      for (const fk of fks) {
        if (fk.table === targetTable) {
          console.log(`Table: ${t.name} -> Column: ${fk.from} references ${fk.table}(${fk.to})`);
          const cnt = (db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM "${t.name}" WHERE "${fk.from}" IS NOT NULL`).get() as any).c;
          console.log(`   └ Current records with non-null FK: ${cnt}`);
        }
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`Error checking ${t.name}:`, e.message);
    }
  }
}

checkForeignKeys('local_data_houday.sqlite', 'houday_vehicles');
checkForeignKeys('local_data_gh.sqlite', 'gh_units');

import { db } from '../src/lib/db';

console.log('=== All tables in DB ===');
const tables = db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all().map((r: any) => r.name);
console.log(tables.join(', '));

console.log('\n=== Contractor agreements count ===');
try {
  const contractors = db.prepare("SELECT count(*) as count FROM contractor_agreements").get() as any;
  console.log('contractor_agreements:', contractors.count);
  const sample = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM contractor_agreements LIMIT 5").all();
  console.log('Sample contractors:', sample);
} catch (e: any) {
  console.log('Error querying contractor_agreements:', e.message);
}

console.log('\n=== Offsite workplaces count ===');
try {
  const workplaces = db.prepare("SELECT count(*) as count FROM offsite_workplaces").get() as any;
  console.log('offsite_workplaces:', workplaces.count);
  const sample = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM offsite_workplaces LIMIT 5").all();
  console.log('Sample workplaces:', sample);
} catch (e: any) {
  console.log('Error querying offsite_workplaces:', e.message);
}

console.log('=== Recent offsite months ===');
const recentMonths = db.prepare(`
  SELECT substr(record_date, 1, 7) as ym, count(*) as count 
  FROM attendance_records 
  WHERE is_offsite = 1 
  GROUP BY ym 
  ORDER BY ym DESC 
  LIMIT 10
`).all();
console.log(recentMonths);

import http from 'http';

http.get('http://localhost:3000/billing/offsite-report?month=2026-09', res => {
  let data = '';
  res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
  res.on('end', () => {
    const ths = data.match(/<th[^>]*>(.*?)<\/th>/g);
    console.log('TH items:', ths?.slice(0, 10));
    const h2s = data.match(/<span[^>]*class="[^"]*font-bold[^"]*"[^>]*>(.*?)<\/span>/g);
    console.log('Spans:', h2s?.slice(0, 10));
  });
});

const tablesToTruncate = [

================================================================================
File: scripts/deep_check.js
================================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function getTsxFiles(dir) {
  let results = [];
  const list = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  list.forEach(file => {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, file);
    const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    if (stat && stat.isDirectory()) {
      results = results.concat(getTsxFiles(fullPath));
    } else if (file.endsWith('.tsx')) {
      results.push(fullPath);
    }
  });
  return results;
}

const files = getTsxFiles(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'app'));

const severeIssues = [];

files.forEach(file => {
  const relPath = path.relative(path.join(__dirname, '..'), file);
  const content = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  const lines = content.split('\n');

  lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
    const lineNum = idx + 1;

    // 1. Double property access on nullable without optional chaining, e.g. client.contract.hourly_wage
    // match: a.b.c where neither has ?.
    const dotChain = line.match(/\b([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\.([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\.([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\b/g);
    if (dotChain) {
      dotChain.forEach(chain => {
        // filter out common safe chains
        if (!chain.startsWith('process.env') && 
            !chain.startsWith('React.') && 
            !chain.startsWith('e.target.') && 
            !chain.startsWith('e.currentTarget.') &&
            !chain.startsWith('Date.') &&
            !chain.startsWith('Math.') &&
            !chain.startsWith('JSON.') &&
            !chain.startsWith('console.') &&
            !chain.includes('target_month') &&
            !chain.includes('length')) {
          
          // If the root object is client, record, item, user, data, slip, etc.
          const root = chain.split('.')[0];
          if (['client', 'record', 'item', 'user', 'data', 'slip', 'plan', 'attendance'].includes(root)) {
            // Check if line has optional chaining or null check
            if (!line.includes(`${root}?.`) && !line.includes(`${root} &&`) && !line.includes(`if (${root})`)) {
              severeIssues.push({
                type: 'Unsafe nested property access',
                severity: 'HIGH',
                file: relPath,
                line: lineNum,
                code: chain,
                fullLine: line.trim()
              });
            }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    // 2. new Date() without validation before getTime / getFullYear etc.
    const dateMethodMatch = line.match(/new Date\(([^)]+)\)\.(getTime|getFullYear|getMonth|getDate|toISOString)\(\)/);
    if (dateMethodMatch) {
      const arg = dateMethodMatch[1].trim();
      if (arg && arg !== '' && !arg.startsWith('"') && !arg.startsWith("'")) {
        // e.g. new Date(item.date).getTime()
        // Check if guarded by ternary or if
        if (!line.includes(`${arg} ?`) && !line.includes(`if (${arg})`) && !line.includes(`${arg} &&`)) {
          severeIssues.push({
            type: 'Unsafe new Date method call',
            severity: 'HIGH',
            file: relPath,
            line: lineNum,
            code: dateMethodMatch[0],
            fullLine: line.trim()
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Array indexing followed by property access without check: arr[0].something
    const arrayIndexMatch = line.match(/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\[[0-9]+\]\.([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/);
    if (arrayIndexMatch && !line.includes('?.') && !line.includes('&&') && !line.includes('if (')) {
      const arrName = arrayIndexMatch[1];
      if (!['split', 'parts', 'matches'].includes(arrName)) {
        severeIssues.push({
          type: 'Unsafe array index property access',
          severity: 'HIGH',
          file: relPath,
          line: lineNum,
          code: arrayIndexMatch[0],
          fullLine: line.trim()
        });
      }
    }
  });
});

console.log(`Deep check completed. Found ${severeIssues.length} severe issue candidates.`);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'deep_check_results.json'), JSON.stringify(severeIssues, null, 2));

  const psOutput = execSync('powershell "Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \\"name=\'cloudflared.exe\'\\" | Select-Object ProcessId, CommandLine"').toString();
  console.log('Cloudflared process:', psOutput);
} catch (e: any) {
  console.log('Error checking process:', e.message);
}

}

const http = require('http');

http.get('http://localhost:3000/login', (res) => {
  let data = '';
  res.on('data', c => data += c);
  res.on('end', () => {
    console.log('Login page length:', data.length);
    // フォームのアクションやhidden inputを確認
    const inputs = data.match(/<input[^>]*>/g) || [];
    console.log('Inputs found:', inputs);
  });
});


================================================================================
File: scripts/list_tables_and_counts.ts
================================================================================

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import path from 'path';

const dbPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data.sqlite');
const db = new Database(dbPath);

const tables = db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").all() as { name: string }[];

console.log('--- テーブル別レコード数 ---');
for (const t of tables) {
  try {
    const res = db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) as count FROM "${t.name}"`).get() as { count: number };
    if (res.count > 0) {
      console.log(`${t.name}: ${res.count} 件`);
    }
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.log(`${t.name}: エラー (${e.message})`);
  }
}

const db = require('better-sqlite3')('local_data.sqlite');
db.exec(`
-- 1. フェイスシート
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_facesheets (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  family_structure TEXT,
  medical_history TEXT,
  disability_details TEXT,
  emergency_contact TEXT,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- 2. アセスメント
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessments (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  assessment_date TEXT NOT NULL,
  client_hopes TEXT,
  current_challenges TEXT,
  working_capabilities TEXT,
  consent_date TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- 3. モニタリング
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monitorings (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  plan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  monitoring_date TEXT NOT NULL,
  evaluation TEXT,
  next_steps TEXT,
  consent_date TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (plan_id) REFERENCES support_plans(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- 4. 雇用契約・採用情報
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS employment_contracts (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  hire_date TEXT,
  contract_start_date TEXT,
  contract_end_date TEXT,
  working_hours_per_week REAL,
  hourly_wage INTEGER,
  duties TEXT,
  consent_date TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
`);

// 5. support_plans に consent_date を追加
try {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE support_plans ADD COLUMN consent_date TEXT;`);
  console.log('Added consent_date to support_plans');
} catch (e) {
  if (e.message.includes('duplicate column name')) {
    console.log('consent_date already exists in support_plans');
  } else {
    throw e;
  }
}

console.log('Migration PDCA successful');

const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
const path = require('path');

const dbPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'local_data.sqlite');
const db = new Database(dbPath);

console.log('Starting Phase 6 Migration...');

try {
  // incident_reports (ヒヤリハット・事故報告書)
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS incident_reports (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      incident_date TEXT,
      incident_time TEXT,
      location TEXT,
      situation TEXT,
      cause TEXT,
      prevention TEXT,
      reporter TEXT,
      created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    )
  `);
  console.log('Created incident_reports table');

  // absence_logs (欠席時対応記録)
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS absence_logs (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      absence_date TEXT,
      contact_person TEXT,
      response_details TEXT,
      created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    )
  `);
  console.log('Created absence_logs table');

  // interview_records (面談・相談記録)
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS interview_records (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      interview_date TEXT,
      topic TEXT,
      notes TEXT,
      created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    )
  `);
  console.log('Created interview_records table');

  console.log('Migration completed successfully!');
} catch (error) {
  console.error('Migration failed:', error);
} finally {
  db.close();
}

================================================================================
File: scripts/seed_full_pdca_and_compliance.ts
================================================================================

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import path from 'path';

const dbPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data.sqlite');
const db = new Database(dbPath);

console.log('=== 6年間PDCA書類・個別支援計画（本案）・モニタリング・行政提出書類の完全記入シード開始 ===');

// 1. 個別支援計画書（本案）の完全記入
console.log('1. 個別支援計画書（本案）のデータ補完中...');
const plans = db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM support_plans`).all() as any[];

const updatePlanStmt = db.prepare(`
  UPDATE support_plans SET
    domains_data = ?,
    creator_name = ?,
    consent_date = ?,
    delivery_date = ?,
    monitoring_period = ?,
    signature_file_path = ?,
    updated_at = datetime('now')
  WHERE id = ?
`);

const transitionPhases = [
  '準備フェーズ（基礎的な労働習慣の定着）',
  '準備フェーズ（基礎的な労働習慣の定着）',
  '求職活動フェーズ（ハローワーク登録・面接練習）',
  '求職活動フェーズ（ハローワーク登録・面接練習）',
  '移行フェーズ（企業実習・トライアル雇用）',
  '定着支援フェーズ（一般就労後のフォロー）'
];

db.transaction(() => {
  for (const p of plans) {
    let rawData: any = {};
    try {
      rawData = JSON.parse(p.domains_data || '{}');
    } catch (e) {}

    // 年次（何年目の計画か）を推定
    const planYear = parseInt(p.plan_date ? p.plan_date.slice(0, 4) : '2020');
    const yearIndex = Math.min(Math.max(planYear - 2020, 0), 5);
    const phase = transitionPhases[yearIndex];

    const consentDate = p.consent_date || (p.plan_date ? p.plan_date : '2020-04-01');
    const deliveryDate = p.delivery_date || consentDate;

    // 実地指導・本案水準の本格的かつ個別的なデータセット（camelCaseとsnake_case双方を完全格納）
    const wishes = rawData.userWishes || rawData.client_request || 
      (yearIndex < 2 
        ? '生活リズムを整え、毎日の作業に休まず通所できるようになりたい。作業の手順を確実に覚えたい。' 
        : yearIndex < 4 
          ? '施設外就労や新しい軽作業に挑戦し、作業スピードと正確性を向上させたい。周囲とのコミュニケーションを円滑にしたい。' 
          : '一般企業への就職を目指し、企業実習や面接練習に取り組みたい。PC入力や事務補助のスキルも身につけたい。');

    const lifeIssues = rawData.lifeChallenges || rawData.life_issues || 
      '就寝・起床リズムの安定化と毎日の服薬管理の徹底。週末の適切な休養とストレス発散方法の確立。';

    const workIssues = rawData.workChallenges || rawData.work_issues || 
      '作業開始時の手順書確認と疑問点が生じた際の速やかな報連相。検品作業における見落とし防止とチェックシートの確実な活用。';

    const generalEmployment = rawData.generalEmploymentGoal || rawData.transition_to_general_employment || 
      (yearIndex < 2 
        ? '事業所内での基礎的労働習慣の定着（挨拶・身だしなみ・時間厳守・指示受け）。' 
        : yearIndex < 4 
          ? '施設外就労先企業での実地作業を通じた職場適応力の養成と体力・持続力の向上。' 
          : 'ハローワーク障害者専門窓口への定期同行、模擬面接練習、および受入れ候補企業での職場実習参加。');

    const longTermGoal = rawData.longTermGoal || rawData.long_term_goal || 
      (yearIndex < 2 
        ? '1年間無遅刻無欠勤での通所を達成し、所定作業を指導員の補助なしで完遂できる自信をつける。' 
        : yearIndex < 4 
          ? '施設外就労グループにおいて副リーダー的な役割を担い、チーム全体の作業効率向上に貢献する。' 
          : '一般企業の障害者雇用枠（事務・軽作業）へ応募し、正社員または契約社員として就職・職場定着を果たす。');

    const shortTermGoal = rawData.shortTermGoal || rawData.short_term_goal || 
      (yearIndex < 2 
        ? '今後6ヶ月間、月間出席率95%以上を維持し、部品仕分け作業の1日の標準ノルマを安定して達成する。' 
        : yearIndex < 4 
          ? '施設外就労（清掃・検品作業）に週2回以上参加し、企業担当者からの指示を正確にメモして実行できる。' 
          : '履歴書・職務経歴書の作成を完了し、月1回以上の企業見学会または職場体験実習に参加する。');

    const targetPeriod = p.end_date || '6ヶ月後';

    const supportDetails = rawData.supportDetails || rawData.support_content || 
      '【職業指導】作業工程の視覚化マニュアルの提供、作業開始前の個別オリエンテーション、および日次の出来高・品質チェック。\n' +
      '【生活支援】週1回の体調確認面談、服薬チェックシートの共有、および就労継続に関する不安やストレスの傾聴・助言。\n' +
      '【サビ管支援】半期ごとの個別支援計画見直し会議の主宰、定期モニタリング面談の実施、および相談支援専門員・医療機関・就労移行機関とのケース会議調整。';

    const staffRole = '職業指導員・生活支援員・サービス管理責任者';
    const policy = rawData.overallPolicy || rawData.general_policy || 
      '本人の得意分野や強みを活かし、小さな成功体験を積み重ねることで自己効力感を高める。心身の負担に配慮しながら段階的に作業負荷を調整し、自立に向けた意欲を尊重する支援を行う。';

    const enrichedDomains = {
      // camelCase
      userWishes: wishes,
      lifeChallenges: lifeIssues,
      workChallenges: workIssues,
      generalEmploymentGoal: generalEmployment,
      longTermGoal: longTermGoal,
      shortTermGoal: shortTermGoal,
      shortTermGoalTargetPeriod: targetPeriod,
      supportDetails: supportDetails,
      supportStaffRole: staffRole,
      overallPolicy: policy,
      employmentPhase: phase,
      // snake_case (PlanViewer互換)
      client_request: wishes,
      life_issues: lifeIssues,
      work_issues: workIssues,
      transition_to_general_employment: generalEmployment,
      long_term_goal: longTermGoal,
      short_term_goal: shortTermGoal,
      target_achievement_date: targetPeriod,
      support_content: supportDetails,
      responsible_staff: staffRole,
      general_policy: policy,
      transition_phase: phase
    };

    updatePlanStmt.run(
      JSON.stringify(enrichedDomains),
      '有馬 誠（専従サビ管）',
      consentDate,
      deliveryDate,
      '6ヶ月',
      '/signatures/plan_agreed.png',
      p.id
    );
  }
})();
console.log(`個別支援計画書（本案）${plans.length}件のデータ補完完了。`);

// 2. 定期モニタリングの完全記入
console.log('2. 定期モニタリングのデータ補完中...');
const monitorings = db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM plan_monitorings`).all() as any[];

const updateMonStmt = db.prepare(`
  UPDATE plan_monitorings SET
    progress_status = ?,
    goal_achievement = ?,
    client_feedback = ?,
    staff_evaluation = ?,
    next_action_plan = ?,
    evaluator_name = ?,
    updated_at = datetime('now')
  WHERE id = ?
`);

db.transaction(() => {
  for (const m of monitorings) {
    const monDate = m.monitoring_date || '2020-10-01';
    const monYear = parseInt(monDate.slice(0, 4));
    const yearIdx = Math.min(Math.max(monYear - 2020, 0), 5);

    const goalAchievement = yearIdx < 2
      ? '短期目標の通所安定度を概ね達成（出席率92%）。作業手順の理解も深まり、基本作業を一人で進められる時間が増加した（目標達成率85%）。'
      : yearIdx < 4
        ? '施設外就労での検品・清掃作業に積極的に取り組み、目標ノルマを安定してクリア。作業エラーも前月比大幅減（目標達成率90%）。'
        : '一般就労に向けた面接練習および求人票選定が順調に進行。PC操作スキルも向上し、実習受け入れ先からも高評価（目標達成率95%）。';

    const clientFeedback = yearIdx < 2
      ? '「毎日通うのが習慣になり、朝起きるのが辛くなくなりました。指導員さんが優しく教えてくれるので安心できます。」'
      : yearIdx < 4
        ? '「施設外就労で外の会社に行って作業するのが良い気分転換になっています。もっとスピードを上げられるように頑張りたいです。」'
        : '「ハローワークの面接会に参加してみて、一般就職への現実味が出てきました。履歴書の志望動機をサビ管さんと推敲しています。」';

    const staffEvaluation = yearIdx < 2
      ? '健康管理への意識が高まり、欠勤の際は事前に連絡を入れる等、基本的なビジネスマナーが身についている。丁寧な作業姿勢が評価できる。'
      : yearIdx < 4
        ? 'チーム内での協調性が向上し、他の利用者への声かけや作業工程の補助も自発的に行うなど、頼もしい成長が見られる。'
        : '自己の障害特性に対する理解が深まり、疲労やストレス時のセルフケアが確立できている。一般就労への準備状況は極めて良好。';

    const nextAction = yearIdx < 2
      ? '次期計画では作業難易度を一段階引き上げ、施設外就労の体験参加を組み込む。体調管理の継続支援を実施する。'
      : yearIdx < 4
        ? '施設外就労の参加日数を週2日から週3日へ拡大し、より実践的な職場環境での適応力を高める。'
        : '企業実習の具体的な日程調整とハローワーク同行面接の実施。定着支援事業所との事前面談を設定する。';

    updateMonStmt.run(
      'smooth',
      goalAchievement,
      clientFeedback,
      staffEvaluation,
      nextAction,
      '有馬 誠（サービス管理責任者）',
      m.id
    );
  }
})();
console.log(`定期モニタリング${monitorings.length}件のデータ補完完了。`);

// 3. 施設外就労評価表の完全記入
console.log('3. 施設外就労評価表のデータ補完中...');
const evals = db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM offsite_evaluations`).all() as any[];

  UPDATE offsite_evaluations SET
    basic_habits_score = ?,
    work_performance_score = ?,
    interpersonal_score = ?,
    safety_score = ?,
    readiness_score = ?,

    supervisor_name = ?,
    feedback_date = ?,

  WHERE id = ?
`);

db.transaction(() => {
  for (const ev of evals) {
    const basicHabits = 5;
    const workPerf = 4;
    const interPers = 5;
    const safety = 5;
    const readiness = 5;

    const tasks = ev.work_tasks || '部品検品・梱包作業、作業エリアの清掃および整理整頓';
    const detailed = '請負先企業の現場責任者様からも、作業の丁寧さと真面目な勤務態度を高くご評価いただいております。指示受け時のメモ取りと復唱確認が習慣化されており、不良品の発生率はゼロでした。';
    const strengths = '集中力が持続し、周囲との協調的な声かけや挨拶が自然にできています。作業手順書の確認を怠らず、自ら品質維持に努める姿勢が見られます。';
    const challenges = '作業速度をさらに10%向上させること、および手順変更時に落ち着いて優先順位を判断できるように引き続き指導員がサポートします。';
    const planRef = '次期の個別支援計画（本案）において、施設外就労の従事日数を拡大し、チーム作業でのリーダー補助業務を目標として設定します。';
    const fbDate = ev.evaluation_date || '2026-08-31';

    updateEvalStmt.run(
      basicHabits, workPerf, interPers, safety, readiness,
      tasks, detailed, strengths, challenges, planRef,
      '須磨 太郎（職業指導員）',
      '有馬 誠（サービス管理責任者）',
      fbDate,
      '本人同意・確認署名済',
      ev.id
    );
  }
})();
console.log(`施設外就労評価表${evals.length}件のデータ補完完了。`);

// 4. 生産活動収支（production_activities）の連動更新（78ヶ月分）
console.log('4. 生産活動収支報告データ（全78ヶ月分）の連動更新中...');
const payrollMonths = db.prepare(`
  SELECT target_month, SUM(total_payment) as total_wages
  FROM monthly_payslips
  GROUP BY target_month
  ORDER BY target_month ASC
`).all() as any[];

const upsertProdStmt = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO production_activities (id, target_month, total_revenue, total_expenses, total_wages_paid, updated_at)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
  ON CONFLICT(target_month) DO UPDATE SET
    total_revenue = excluded.total_revenue,
    total_expenses = excluded.total_expenses,
    total_wages_paid = excluded.total_wages_paid,
    updated_at = datetime('now')
`);

db.transaction(() => {
  for (const pm of payrollMonths) {
    const month = pm.target_month;
    const wages = pm.total_wages || 1500000;
    // 健全経営基準: 売上 - 経費 >= 賃金総額
    // 売上を賃金の約1.35倍、経費を賃金の約0.25倍に設定（差額 = 1.1倍 >= 1.0倍）
    const revenue = Math.round(wages * 1.35);
    const expenses = Math.round(wages * 0.25);
    const id = `prod_${month}`;

    upsertProdStmt.run(id, month, revenue, expenses, wages);
  }
})();
console.log(`生産活動収支報告データ${payrollMonths.length}ヶ月分の更新完了。`);

// 5. サービス提供実績記録票（monthly_provision_records）の整備
console.log('5. サービス提供実績記録票（全利用者の月次確定ロックデータ）整備中...');
const allClients = db.prepare(`SELECT id FROM clients`).all() as any[];
const allMonths = payrollMonths.map((p: any) => p.target_month);

const insertProvStmt = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO monthly_provision_records (id, client_id, target_month, signature_data, is_locked, created_at, updated_at)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))
  ON CONFLICT(client_id, target_month) DO UPDATE SET
    is_locked = 1,
    signature_data = excluded.signature_data,
    updated_at = datetime('now')
`);

db.transaction(() => {
  for (const c of allClients) {
    for (const m of allMonths) {
      const id = `prov_${c.id}_${m}`;
      insertProvStmt.run(id, c.id, m, 'data:image/png;base64,sample_signature');
    }
  }
})();
console.log(`サービス提供実績記録票 ${allClients.length * allMonths.length}件の整備完了。`);

console.log('=== 全てのPDCA書類・行政提出書類のシード完了 ===');

    statement: '「先週のハローワークで紹介された物流企業のデータ入力・仕分け求人に興味があります。実習に行って自分の力が通用するか試してみたいです。」',
    response: '求人条件（勤務時間・通勤アクセス・配慮事項）を精査。障害者就業・生活支援センターおよびハローワークと連携し、2週間の職場体験実習の受け入れ調整を行うことを決定した。',
    nextAction: '企業担当者との事前打合せを設定。履歴書および職務経歴書の推敲をサビ管と週1回実施する。',
    interviewer: '有馬 誠（専従サビ管）',
    location: '相談室（事業所内）'
  }
];

let totalInterviewsCreated = 0;

db.transaction(() => {
  // 既存のinterview_recordsを一旦クリーンアップ
  db.prepare(`DELETE FROM interview_records`).run();

  for (const client of clients) {
    const startDate = client.service_start_date || client.contract_date || '2020-04-01';
    const endDate = client.contract_end_date || '2026-09-30';
    
    const startYear = parseInt(startDate.slice(0, 4));
    const endYear = parseInt(endDate.slice(0, 4));

    // 各年次ごとに2〜3回の面談を生成
    for (let yr = startYear; yr <= Math.min(endYear, 2026); yr++) {
      const yearIndex = yr - 2020; // 0: 2020, 1: 2021, ... 6: 2026

      // 年次に応じたテンプレートの選択
      let templateIndices: number[] = [];
      if (yearIndex === 0) {
        templateIndices = [0, 1, 2];
      } else if (yearIndex === 1 || yearIndex === 2) {
        templateIndices = [2, 3, 4];
      } else if (yearIndex === 3 || yearIndex === 4) {
        templateIndices = [4, 5, 6, 7];
      } else {
        templateIndices = [6, 7, 8, 9];
      }

      // 年に2〜3回
      for (let i = 0; i < templateIndices.length; i++) {
        const tIdx = templateIndices[i];
        const tmpl = interviewTemplates[tIdx];

        // 面談月を分散（例: 4月, 8月, 11月等）
        const monthNum = 3 + (i * 4);
        const mStr = String(Math.min(monthNum, 12)).padStart(2, '0');
        const dayStr = String(10 + (i * 5)).padStart(2, '0');
        const interviewDate = `${yr}-${mStr}-${dayStr}`;

        // 契約終了日を超えている場合はスキップ
        if (interviewDate > endDate) continue;

        // 次回面談日（約2〜3ヶ月後）
        const nextMonth = Math.min(monthNum + 3, 12);
        const nextDate = `${yr}-${String(nextMonth).padStart(2, '0')}-15`;

        const id = `interview_${client.id}_${yr}_${i + 1}`;

        insertInterviewStmt.run(
          id,
          client.id,
          interviewDate,
          tmpl.topic,
          tmpl.statement, // notes
          tmpl.type,
          tmpl.interviewer,
          tmpl.location,
          tmpl.statement,
          tmpl.response,
          tmpl.nextAction,
          nextDate,
          tmpl.attendees || null
        );

        totalInterviewsCreated++;
      }
    }
  }
})();

console.log(`=== 6年間 個別面談・相談援助記録 ${totalInterviewsCreated} 件のシード完了 ===`);

    contract_amount, unit_price, expected_monthly_revenue, assigned_clients_count, notes
  ) VALUES (
    @id, @company_name, @work_description, @contract_start_date, @contract_end_date,
    @contract_amount, @unit_price, @expected_monthly_revenue, @assigned_clients_count, @notes
  )
  ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
    company_name = excluded.company_name,
    work_description = excluded.work_description,
    contract_start_date = excluded.contract_start_date,
    contract_end_date = excluded.contract_end_date,
    contract_amount = excluded.contract_amount,
    unit_price = excluded.unit_price,
    expected_monthly_revenue = excluded.expected_monthly_revenue,
    assigned_clients_count = excluded.assigned_clients_count,
    notes = excluded.notes
`);

`).all() as any[];

  UPDATE attendance_records 
  SET 
    offsite_workplace_id = CASE 
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%1' OR client_id LIKE '%4' OR client_id LIKE '%7' THEN 'workplace_01'
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%2' OR client_id LIKE '%5' OR client_id LIKE '%8' THEN 'workplace_02'
      ELSE 'workplace_03'
    END,
    offsite_staff_id = CASE 
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%1' OR client_id LIKE '%4' OR client_id LIKE '%7' THEN ?
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%2' OR client_id LIKE '%5' OR client_id LIKE '%8' THEN ?
      ELSE ?
    END,
    visit_staff_name = CASE 
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%1' OR client_id LIKE '%4' OR client_id LIKE '%7' THEN ?
      WHEN client_id LIKE '%2' OR client_id LIKE '%5' OR client_id LIKE '%8' THEN ?
      ELSE ?
    END
  WHERE is_offsite = 1
`).run(
  defaultStaff1.id, defaultStaff2.id, defaultStaff3.id,
  defaultStaff1.name, defaultStaff2.name, defaultStaff3.name
);

const updatedCount = db.prepare("SELECT count(*) as count FROM attendance_records WHERE is_offsite = 1 AND offsite_workplace_id IS NOT NULL").get() as any;
console.log(`✅ 施設外就労勤怠レコード ${updatedCount.count} 件 の紐付け完了！`);

// 5. 施設外就労評価表（offsite_evaluations）データの6年間シミュレーション生成
console.log('🔄 施設外就労評価表データの生成中...');

// 施設外就労に従事した利用者リストを取得
const offsiteClients = db.prepare(`
  SELECT DISTINCT c.id, c.full_name
  FROM attendance_records a
  JOIN clients c ON a.client_id = c.id
  WHERE a.is_offsite = 1
  ORDER BY c.id
`).all() as any[];

// 半期ごとの評価期間（2020年前期〜2026年前期まで計13期）
const evalPeriods = [
  { pStart: '2020-04-01', pEnd: '2020-09-30', evalDate: '2020-10-05', feedbackDate: '2020-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2020-10-01', pEnd: '2021-03-31', evalDate: '2021-04-05', feedbackDate: '2021-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2021-04-01', pEnd: '2021-09-30', evalDate: '2021-10-05', feedbackDate: '2021-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2021-10-01', pEnd: '2022-03-31', evalDate: '2022-04-05', feedbackDate: '2022-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2022-04-01', pEnd: '2022-09-30', evalDate: '2022-10-05', feedbackDate: '2022-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2022-10-01', pEnd: '2023-03-31', evalDate: '2023-04-05', feedbackDate: '2023-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2023-04-01', pEnd: '2023-09-30', evalDate: '2023-10-05', feedbackDate: '2023-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2023-10-01', pEnd: '2024-03-31', evalDate: '2024-04-05', feedbackDate: '2024-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2024-04-01', pEnd: '2024-09-30', evalDate: '2024-10-05', feedbackDate: '2024-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2024-10-01', pEnd: '2025-03-31', evalDate: '2025-04-05', feedbackDate: '2025-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2025-04-01', pEnd: '2025-09-30', evalDate: '2025-10-05', feedbackDate: '2025-10-10' },
  { pStart: '2025-10-01', pEnd: '2026-03-31', evalDate: '2026-04-05', feedbackDate: '2026-04-10' },
  { pStart: '2026-04-01', pEnd: '2026-09-30', evalDate: '2026-09-15', feedbackDate: '2026-09-18' }
];

const insertEvaluation = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO offsite_evaluations (
    id, client_id, workplace_id, contractor_id, evaluation_date, period_start, period_end,
    work_tasks, basic_habits_score, work_performance_score, interpersonal_score, safety_score, readiness_score,
    detailed_evaluation, strengths_and_growth, challenges_and_support, plan_reflection,
    evaluator_id, evaluator_name, supervisor_id, supervisor_name, feedback_date, client_signature
  ) VALUES (
    @id, @client_id, @workplace_id, @contractor_id, @evaluation_date, @period_start, @period_end,
    @work_tasks, @basic_habits_score, @work_performance_score, @interpersonal_score, @safety_score, @readiness_score,
    @detailed_evaluation, @strengths_and_growth, @challenges_and_support, @plan_reflection,
    @evaluator_id, @evaluator_name, @supervisor_id, @supervisor_name, @feedback_date, @client_signature
  )
  ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
    work_tasks = excluded.work_tasks,
    basic_habits_score = excluded.basic_habits_score,
    work_performance_score = excluded.work_performance_score,
    interpersonal_score = excluded.interpersonal_score,
    safety_score = excluded.safety_score,
    readiness_score = excluded.readiness_score,
    detailed_evaluation = excluded.detailed_evaluation,
    strengths_and_growth = excluded.strengths_and_growth,
    challenges_and_support = excluded.challenges_and_support,
    plan_reflection = excluded.plan_reflection
`);

let evalCount = 0;

for (const client of offsiteClients) {
  // 利用者の就労先
  const wpIndex = (parseInt(client.id.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') || '1') % 3);
  const targetWp = workplaces[wpIndex];
  const targetStaff = [defaultStaff1, defaultStaff2, defaultStaff3][wpIndex];

  // 評価期間ごとに評価表を作成
  for (let i = 0; i < evalPeriods.length; i++) {
    const period = evalPeriods[i];
    const evalId = `eval_${client.id}_p${i + 1}`;

    // 契約IDの選定
    let contractorId = 'contract_01_2020';
    if (wpIndex === 0) {
      contractorId = period.pStart < '2023-04-01' ? 'contract_01_2020' : 'contract_01_2023';
    } else if (wpIndex === 1) {
      contractorId = period.pStart < '2023-04-01' ? 'contract_02_2020' : 'contract_02_2023';
    } else {
      contractorId = period.pStart < '2024-04-01' ? 'contract_03_2021' : 'contract_03_2024';
    }

    // スコアは年数経過とともに3から4, 5へ徐々に成長
    const growthBonus = Math.min(2, Math.floor(i / 4));
    const basicScore = Math.min(5, 3 + (i % 2) + (growthBonus > 0 ? 1 : 0));
    const perfScore = Math.min(5, 3 + growthBonus);
    const interScore = Math.min(5, 3 + Math.floor(i / 5));
    const safeScore = 4;
    const readyScore = Math.min(5, 3 + growthBonus);

    insertEvaluation.run({
      id: evalId,
      client_id: client.id,
      workplace_id: targetWp.id,
      contractor_id: contractorId,
      evaluation_date: period.evalDate,
      period_start: period.pStart,
      period_end: period.pEnd,
      work_tasks: targetWp.tasks,
      basic_habits_score: basicScore,
      work_performance_score: perfScore,
      interpersonal_score: interScore,
      safety_score: safeScore,
      readiness_score: readyScore,
      detailed_evaluation: `${targetWp.name}での施設外就労に従事。業務手順を忠実に守り、丁寧な作業姿勢を維持できています。企業先の担当者からも「欠勤・遅刻がなく真面目に取り組んでいる」と高評価を得ています。`,
      strengths_and_growth: `期初と比較して作業スピードが約20%向上し、エラー率も低下しました。不明点や作業の区切りには自分から同行指導員へ報告できるようになりました。`,
      challenges_and_support: `連続した立ち作業時に集中力が途切れる傾向があるため、適切な水分補給と1時間ごとの軽いストレッチを指導員から声掛けサポートします。`,
      plan_reflection: `次期個別支援計画において、施設外就労の従事日数を週3日から週4日へステップアップし、一般就労移行に向けた自己体調管理の自立を長期目標に反映します。`,
      evaluator_id: targetStaff.id,
      evaluator_name: targetStaff.name,
      supervisor_id: sabikan.id,
      supervisor_name: sabikan.name,
      feedback_date: period.feedbackDate,
      client_signature: '本人署名・合意済'
    });

    evalCount++;
  }
}

console.log(`✅ 施設外就労評価表（offsite_evaluations）計 ${evalCount} 件 生成・保存完了！`);
console.log('🎉 施設外就労・請負契約の全データ投入が正常終了しました！');

================================================================================
File: scripts/start-tunnel.ts
================================================================================

import { spawn, execSync } from 'child_process';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

// 1. 既存の cloudflared を終了
try {
  execSync('taskkill /F /IM cloudflared.exe', { stdio: 'ignore' });
  console.log('Terminated existing cloudflared.');
} catch (e) {
  // 実行されていなければOK
}

const cloudflaredPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'cloudflared.exe');
console.log('Starting cloudflared from:', cloudflaredPath);

const logStream = fs.createWriteStream(path.join(process.cwd(), 'cloudflared_tunnel.log'), { flags: 'w' });

const proc = spawn(cloudflaredPath, ['tunnel', '--url', 'http://localhost:3000'], {
  detached: true,
  stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
});

let foundUrl = '';

proc.stdout?.on('data', (data) => {
  const str = data.toString();
  logStream.write(str);
  checkForUrl(str);
});

proc.stderr?.on('data', (data) => {
  const str = data.toString();
  logStream.write(str);
  checkForUrl(str);
});

function checkForUrl(output: string) {
  const match = output.match(/https:\/\/[a-z0-9-]+\.trycloudflare\.com/);
  if (match && !foundUrl) {
    foundUrl = match[0];
    console.log('\n======================================================');
    console.log('🎉 Public Tunnel URL Found:');
    console.log(foundUrl);
    console.log('======================================================\n');
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), '.tunnel_url'), foundUrl);
  }
}

// 15秒間監視
let elapsed = 0;
const interval = setInterval(() => {
  elapsed += 1;
  if (foundUrl || elapsed >= 15) {
    clearInterval(interval);
    if (!foundUrl) {
      console.error('Timeout waiting for tunnel URL.');
      process.exit(1);
    } else {
      console.log('Tunnel started successfully! Process PID:', proc.pid);
      proc.unref();
      process.exit(0);
    }
  }
}, 1000);


================================================================================
File: scripts/summarize_audit.js
================================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const results = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'audit_results.json'), 'utf8'));

// Group by type and severity
const grouped = {};
results.forEach(r => {
  const key = `${r.severity}: ${r.type}`;
  if (!grouped[key]) grouped[key] = [];
  grouped[key].push(r);
});

console.log('--- SUMMARY BY CATEGORY ---');
Object.keys(grouped).forEach(k => {
  console.log(`${k}: ${grouped[k].length} items`);
});

// Print top issues for each category
console.log('\n--- SAMPLE FINDINGS ---');
Object.keys(grouped).forEach(k => {
  console.log(`\n=== ${k} ===`);
  grouped[k].slice(0, 5).forEach(item => {
    console.log(`- [${item.file}:${item.line}] ${item.code}`);
  });
});

import { getHoudayDb } from '../src/app/(houday)/houday/lib/db';
import { getGhDb } from '../src/app/(gh)/gh/lib/db';
import { deleteHoudayChild } from '../src/app/(houday)/houday/children/actions';
import { deleteResident } from '../src/app/(gh)/gh/residents/actions';

async function testDelete() {
  console.log('=== Testing Houday Child Deletion ===');
  const houdayDb = getHoudayDb();
  
  // 1. テスト児童の登録（子テーブルレコード付き）
  const testChildId = `test_ch_${Date.now()}`;
  houdayDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO houday_children (id, name, furigana, grade, guardian_name, guardian_contact, status)
    VALUES (?, 'テスト児童', 'テストジドウ', '小1', 'テスト保護者', '090-0000-0000', 'active')
  `).run(testChildId);

  // 子テーブルにもダミーデータ挿入
  houdayDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO houday_attendance (id, child_id, target_date, service_type, status)
    VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10-10', 'school_day', 'present')
  `).run(`att_${Date.now()}`, testChildId);

  houdayDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO houday_monthly_billings (id, child_id, target_month, attendance_days, school_day_count, vacation_day_count, snack_count, snack_total_cost, material_cost, transport_count, kokuho_units, guardian_copay_amount, guardian_actual_cost, guardian_grand_total)
    VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10', 1, 1, 0, 1, 100, 1000, 0, 600, 0, 1100, 1100)
  `).run(`bil_${Date.now()}`, testChildId);

  console.log(`Created test child: ${testChildId} with attendance & billing records`);

  // 2. 削除の実行
  const houdayRes = await deleteHoudayChild(testChildId);
  console.log('deleteHoudayChild result:', houdayRes);
  if (!houdayRes.success) {
    throw new Error('Houday child deletion failed: ' + houdayRes.error);
  }
  console.log('✅ Houday Child deleted successfully without FK error!');

  console.log('\n=== Testing GH Resident Deletion ===');
  const ghDb = getGhDb();

  // 3. テスト居室の作成
  const testUnitId = `test_u_${Date.now()}`;
  ghDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO gh_units (id, unit_name, room_number, floor, capacity, is_occupied)
    VALUES (?, 'テスト館', '999', 1, 1, 1)
  `).run(testUnitId);

  // 4. テスト入居者の登録（子テーブルレコード付き）
  const testResId = `test_res_${Date.now()}`;
  ghDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO gh_residents (id, name, furigana, unit_id, room_number, rent_amount, status)
    VALUES (?, 'テスト入居者', 'テストニュウキョシャ', ?, '999', 35000, 'active')
  `).run(testResId, testUnitId);

  // 子テーブルにもダミーデータ挿入
  ghDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO gh_vital_and_meds (id, resident_id, record_date, temperature)
    VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10-10', 36.5)
  `).run(`vit_${Date.now()}`, testResId);

  ghDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO gh_resident_money_ledgers (id, resident_id, transaction_date, transaction_type, category, amount, balance_after)
    VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10-10', 'deposit', '預かり金', 1000, 1000)
  `).run(`mon_${Date.now()}`, testResId);

  ghDb.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO gh_monthly_billings (id, resident_id, target_month, rent_base, rent_subsidy_deduction, rent_net, utilities_cost, supplies_cost, total_resident_payment)
    VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10', 35000, 10000, 25000, 12000, 3000, 40000)
  `).run(`gbil_${Date.now()}`, testResId);

  console.log(`Created test resident: ${testResId} in unit ${testUnitId} with vitals, money, billings`);

  // 5. 削除の実行
  const ghRes = await deleteResident(testResId);
  console.log('deleteResident result:', ghRes);
  if (!ghRes.success) {
    throw new Error('GH resident deletion failed: ' + ghRes.error);
  }

  // 居室が空室（is_occupied = 0）に戻ったか確認
  const unitAfter = ghDb.prepare('SELECT is_occupied FROM gh_units WHERE id = ?').get(testUnitId) as any;
  console.log('Unit after resident deletion is_occupied:', unitAfter.is_occupied);
  if (unitAfter.is_occupied !== 0) {
    throw new Error('Unit was not set back to vacant (0)!');
  }

  // テスト居室のクリーンアップ
  ghDb.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_units WHERE id = ?').run(testUnitId);
  console.log('✅ GH Resident deleted successfully and room returned to vacant without FK error!');

  console.log('\n🎉 ALL DELETE TESTS PASSED PERFECTLY!');
}

testDelete().catch(e => {
  console.error('Test failed:', e);
  process.exit(1);
});

import https from 'https';
import fs from 'fs';

const url = fs.readFileSync('.tunnel_url', 'utf8').trim();
console.log('Testing tunnel URL:', url);

https.get(url, (res) => {
  console.log('Status code:', res.statusCode);
  console.log('Headers:', res.headers);
  let data = '';
  res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
  res.on('end', () => {
    console.log('Response length:', data.length);
    console.log('First 200 chars:', data.slice(0, 200));
  });
}).on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('Fetch error:', err.message);
});


================================================================================
File: scripts/test_settings_isolation.js
================================================================================

const http = require('http');

async function test(name, path, cookie) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const opts = {
      hostname: 'localhost',
      port: 3000,
      path: path,
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {
        'Cookie': 'role=admin; ' + (cookie || '')
      }
    };
    http.get(opts, (res) => {
      let data = '';
      res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
      res.on('end', () => {
        console.log('[' + name + '] Status: ' + res.statusCode + ', Length: ' + data.length);
        resolve({ name, status: res.statusCode, data });
      });
    }).on('error', (err) => {
      console.error('[' + name + '] Error:', err.message);
      resolve({ name, status: 500, error: err.message });
    });
  });
}

(async () => {
  const rLogin = await test('Login Page', '/login', '');
  const rA = await test('Settings Type A', '/settings', 'service_mode=type_a');
  const rB = await test('Settings Type B', '/settings', 'service_mode=type_b');
  const rGh = await test('Settings GH', '/settings', 'service_mode=gh');
  const rHouday = await test('Settings Houday', '/settings', 'service_mode=houday');
  const rCorp = await test('Settings Corporate', '/settings', 'service_mode=corporate');

  console.log('\n--- 混入・分離チェック ---');
  // Type A: 最低賃金が含まれ、目標工賃や居室家賃や送迎車両が含まれないこと
  console.log('Type A has 最低賃金:', rA.data.includes('最低賃金'));
  console.log('Type A has 目標工賃:', rA.data.includes('目標工賃'));
  console.log('Type A has 居室:    ', rA.data.includes('居室'));
  console.log('Type A has おやつ代:', rA.data.includes('おやつ代'));

  // Type B: 目標工賃が含まれ、最低賃金や居室家賃や送迎車両が含まれないこと
  console.log('Type B has 目標工賃:', rB.data.includes('目標工賃'));
  console.log('Type B has 最低賃金:', rB.data.includes('最低賃金'));
  console.log('Type B has 居室:    ', rB.data.includes('居室'));
  console.log('Type B has おやつ代:', rB.data.includes('おやつ代'));

  // GH: 居室・家賃・世話人が含まれ、最低賃金や目標工賃や送迎車両が含まれないこと
  console.log('GH has 居室:        ', rGh.data.includes('居室'));
  console.log('GH has 世話人:      ', rGh.data.includes('世話人'));
  console.log('GH has 最低賃金:    ', rGh.data.includes('最低賃金'));
  console.log('GH has 目標工賃:    ', rGh.data.includes('目標工賃'));

  // Houday: 児童・送迎・おやつ代が含まれ、最低賃金や工賃や家賃が含まれないこと
  console.log('Houday has おやつ代:', rHouday.data.includes('おやつ代'));
  console.log('Houday has 送迎:    ', rHouday.data.includes('送迎'));
  console.log('Houday has 最低賃金:', rHouday.data.includes('最低賃金'));
  console.log('Houday has 目標工賃:', rHouday.data.includes('目標工賃'));

  // Corporate: 4DB・国保連・全銀が含まれ、最低賃金や目標工賃や送迎車両が含まれないこと
  console.log('Corporate has 4DB:   ', rCorp.data.includes('4DB'));
  console.log('Corporate has 全銀:  ', rCorp.data.includes('全銀'));
  console.log('Corporate has 最低賃金:', rCorp.data.includes('最低賃金'));
  console.log('Corporate has 目標工賃:', rCorp.data.includes('目標工賃'));

  // Login: 絵文字（👑等）が含まれないこと
  console.log('Login has 👑:       ', rLogin.data.includes('👑'));
})();

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

function cleanHouday() {
  const db = new Database('local_data_houday.sqlite');
  const before = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM houday_settings').all();
  console.log('Before houday_settings:', before);

  db.prepare(`
    UPDATE houday_settings 
    SET facility_name = '放課後等デイサービス事業所' 
    WHERE facility_name LIKE '%にじいろキッズ%'
  `).run();

  const after = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM houday_settings').all();
  console.log('After houday_settings:', after);
}

function cleanGh() {
  const db = new Database('local_data_gh.sqlite');
  const before = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM gh_settings').all();
  console.log('Before gh_settings:', before);

  db.prepare(`
    UPDATE gh_settings 
    SET facility_name = 'グループホーム（共同生活援助）事業所' 
    WHERE facility_name LIKE '%かがやき荘%'
  `).run();

  const after = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM gh_settings').all();
  console.log('After gh_settings:', after);
}

cleanHouday();
cleanGh();
console.log('Done!');


================================================================================
File: scripts/validate-kokuren-csv.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * 国民健康保険中央会（国保連）インターフェース仕様書 厳格準拠バリデーションスクリプト
 * 
 * 対象サービス:
 * 1. 就労継続支援A型 (サービス種類コード: 43)
 * 2. 就労継続支援B型 (サービス種類コード: 44)
 * 3. 共同生活援助（グループホーム） (サービス種類コード: 33)
 * 4. 放課後等デイサービス (サービス種類コード: 63 - 児童福祉法規格)
 * 
 * 検証項目:
 * - エンコーディング: Shift-JIS (CP932)
 * - 改行コード: CRLF (\r\n)
 * - ヘッダー行なし（レコード01から直接開始）
 * - 各レコード種別（01, 11, 21, 22, 71, 72, 73, 99）のカラム数、型、桁数、パディング、整合性
 */

import {
  generateKokurenCsvForTypeA,
  generateKokurenCsvForTypeB,
  generateKokurenCsvForGh,
  generateKokurenCsvForHouday,
  KokurenCsvResult,
} from '../src/lib/billing/kokurenCsvService';
import {
  saveFacilityAdditions,
  getFacilityAdditions
} from '../src/lib/compliance/facilityAdditionsManager';
import iconv from 'iconv-lite';


interface ValidationError {
  lineIndex: number;
  recordType: string;
  fieldIndex?: number;
  fieldName?: string;
  message: string;
}

interface ValidationReport {
  serviceKey: string;
  serviceCode: string;
  facilityName: string;
  totalRecords: number;
  totalUnits: number;
  totalBillingAmount: number;
  isValid: boolean;
  errors: ValidationError[];
  warnings: string[];
}

function parseCsvLine(line: string): string[] {
  const fields: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  let inQuotes = false;

  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
    const char = line[i];
    if (char === '"') {
      if (inQuotes && line[i + 1] === '"') {
        current += '"';
        i++;
      } else {
        inQuotes = !inQuotes;
      }
    } else if (char === ',' && !inQuotes) {
      fields.push(current);
      current = '';
    } else {
      current += char;
    }
  }
  fields.push(current);
  return fields;
}

function validateKokurenCsv(result: KokurenCsvResult): ValidationReport {
  const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
  const warnings: string[] = [];

  // 1. エンコーディング検証 (Shift-JIS decode test)
  try {
    const decoded = iconv.decode(result.shiftJisBuffer, 'CP932');
    if (!decoded.includes(result.facilityName)) {
      errors.push({
        lineIndex: 0,
        recordType: 'ENCODING',
        message: 'Shift-JISデコード後の文字列に事業所名が正しく含まれていません。',
      });
    }
  } catch (e: any) {
    errors.push({
      lineIndex: 0,
      recordType: 'ENCODING',
      message: `Shift-JISデコードに失敗しました: ${e.message}`,
    });
  }

  // 2. 改行コード検証 (CRLF)
  if (!result.csvText.includes('\r\n')) {
    errors.push({
      lineIndex: 0,
      recordType: 'NEWLINE',
      message: '改行コードが CRLF (\\r\\n) ではありません。',
    });
  }

  const rawLines = result.csvText.split('\r\n');
  // 末尾の空行を除去
  const lines = rawLines.filter((l, idx) => idx < rawLines.length - 1 || l.length > 0);

  // 3. ヘッダー行なし・直接レコード開始検証
  if (lines.length > 0 && !lines[0].startsWith('01,')) {
    errors.push({
      lineIndex: 1,
      recordType: 'HEADER',
      message: '1行目が交換情報レコード（01）から始まっていません。不要なヘッダー行が存在します。',
    });
  }

  let record01Found = false;
  let record11Found = false;
  let record99Found = false;
  let totalDataCount = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const lineNum = i + 1;
    const line = lines[i];
    if (!line.trim()) continue;

    const fields = parseCsvLine(line);
    const recType = fields[0];
    totalDataCount++;

    switch (recType) {
      case '01': {
        // 交換情報レコード: 01,01,事業所番号(10),請求年月(6),作成年月日(8),作成者名称
        record01Found = true;
        if (fields.length !== 6) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '01', message: `カラム数が不正です（期待: 6, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        if (fields[1] !== '01') {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '01', fieldIndex: 1, message: '交換情報識別番号が01ではありません。' });
        }
        if (fields[2]?.length !== 10) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '01', fieldIndex: 2, message: `事業所番号が10桁ではありません（値: ${fields[2]}）` });
        }
        if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(fields[3] || '')) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '01', fieldIndex: 3, message: `請求年月がYYYYMM形式ではありません（値: ${fields[3]}）` });
        }
        if (!/^\d{8}$/.test(fields[4] || '')) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '01', fieldIndex: 4, message: `作成年月日がYYYYMMDD形式ではありません（値: ${fields[4]}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '11': {
        // 請求書レコード: 11,事業所番号(10),サービス種類コード(2),請求年月(6),件数,合計単位数,総費用額,自己負担額,給付費請求額
        record11Found = true;
        if (fields.length !== 9) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '11', message: `カラム数が不正です（期待: 9, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        if (fields[2] !== result.serviceCode) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '11', fieldIndex: 2, message: `サービス種類コードが不正です（期待: ${result.serviceCode}, 実際: ${fields[2]}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '21': {
        // 明細書基本情報: 21,事業所番号(10),受給者証番号(10),サービス種類コード(2),市町村番号(6),請求年月(6),実日数,計画単位,算定単位,総費用,請求額,自己負担
        if (fields.length !== 12) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '21', message: `カラム数が不正です（期待: 12, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        if (fields[2]?.length !== 10) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '21', fieldIndex: 2, message: `受給者証番号が10桁ではありません（値: ${fields[2]}）` });
        }
        if (fields[3] !== result.serviceCode) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '21', fieldIndex: 3, message: `サービス種類コードが不正です（期待: ${result.serviceCode}, 実際: ${fields[3]}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '22': {
        // サービス明細行: 22,事業所番号(10),受給者証番号(10),サービス種類コード(2),サービスコード(4),単位数,回数,合計単位数
        if (fields.length !== 8) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '22', message: `カラム数が不正です（期待: 8, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        if (fields[3] !== result.serviceCode) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '22', fieldIndex: 3, message: `サービス種類コードが不正です（期待: ${result.serviceCode}, 実際: ${fields[3]}）` });
        }
        if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(fields[4] || '')) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '22', fieldIndex: 4, message: `サービスコードが4桁数字ではありません（値: ${fields[4]}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '71': {
        // 実績記録票基本: 71,事業所番号(10),受給者証番号(10),サービス種類コード(2),対象年月(6),実日数
        if (fields.length !== 6) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '71', message: `カラム数が不正です（期待: 6, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        if (fields[3] !== result.serviceCode) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '71', fieldIndex: 3, message: `サービス種類コードが不正です（期待: ${result.serviceCode}, 実際: ${fields[3]}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '72': {
        // サービス提供実績記録票 日別明細
        if (result.serviceCode === '43' || result.serviceCode === '44') {
          // 就労A/B（様式第4）: 72,事業所,受給者証,コード,年月,日,提供状況(1:通所,2:施設外,4:欠席),送迎(0-3),食事(0/1) => 9カラム
          if (fields.length !== 9) {
            errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '72', message: `就労A/B型実績記録票のカラム数が不正です（期待: 9, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
          }
        } else if (result.serviceCode === '33') {
          // GH（別表2）: 72,事業所,受給者証,33,年月,日,入居状況(1/2),帰宅時加算(0/1),朝食(0/1),夕食(0/1) => 10カラム
          if (fields.length !== 10) {
            errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '72', message: `GH実績記録票（別表2）のカラム数が不正です（期待: 10, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
          }
        } else if (result.serviceCode === '63') {
          // 放課後等デイ（児童福祉法 様式第7）: 72,事業所,受給者証,63,年月,日,開学区分(1/2),提供状況(1/2),送迎(0-3),短時間区分(1),医療ケア(0/1) => 11カラム
          if (fields.length !== 11) {
            errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '72', message: `放デイ実績記録票（様式第7）のカラム数が不正です（期待: 11, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      case '73': {
        // 上限額管理結果票: 73,事業所番号,受給者証番号,対象年月,自事業所総費用,他事業所総費用,自事業所負担額,管理結果区分 => 8カラム
        if (fields.length !== 8) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '73', message: `上限額管理結果票のカラム数が不正です（期待: 8, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        break;
      }

      case '99': {
        // トレーラー: 99,総レコード数
        record99Found = true;
        if (fields.length !== 2) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '99', message: `トレーラーのカラム数が不正です（期待: 2, 実際: ${fields.length}）` });
        }
        const reportedCount = parseInt(fields[1], 10);
        if (reportedCount !== totalDataCount) {
          errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: '99', fieldIndex: 1, message: `トレーラーのレコード数(${reportedCount})と実際の総レコード数(${totalDataCount})が一致しません。` });
        }
        break;
      }

      default:
        errors.push({ lineIndex: lineNum, recordType: recType, message: `未定義のレコード種別コードです（値: ${recType}）` });
        break;
    }
  }

  if (!record01Found) errors.push({ lineIndex: 0, recordType: '01', message: '交換情報レコード（01）が見つかりません。' });
  if (!record11Found) errors.push({ lineIndex: 0, recordType: '11', message: '請求書レコード（11）が見つかりません。' });
  if (!record99Found) errors.push({ lineIndex: 0, recordType: '99', message: 'トレーラーレコード（99）が見つかりません。' });

  return {
    serviceKey: result.serviceKey,
    serviceCode: result.serviceCode,
    facilityName: result.facilityName,
    totalRecords: totalDataCount,
    totalUnits: result.totalUnits,
    totalBillingAmount: result.totalBillingAmount,
    isValid: errors.length === 0,
    errors,
    warnings,
  };
}

// =========================================================================
// 実行メイン
// =========================================================================
export async function runKokurenValidation() {
  const targetMonth = '2026-10';
  console.log(`================================================================================`);
  console.log(` 国保連電子請求システム CSVインターフェース仕様書 厳格バリデーションテスト`);
  console.log(` 対象月: ${targetMonth}`);
  console.log(`================================================================================\n`);

  const results: KokurenCsvResult[] = [];

  try {
    console.log(`[1/4] 就労継続支援A型 (サービス種類コード: 43) 生成中...`);
    results.push(generateKokurenCsvForTypeA(targetMonth));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error(`  就労A型 生成エラー: ${e.message}`);
  }

  try {
    console.log(`[2/4] 就労継続支援B型 (サービス種類コード: 44) 生成中...`);
    results.push(generateKokurenCsvForTypeB(targetMonth));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error(`  就労B型 生成エラー: ${e.message}`);
  }

  try {
    console.log(`[3/4] 共同生活援助（グループホーム） (サービス種類コード: 33) 生成中...`);
    results.push(generateKokurenCsvForGh(targetMonth));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error(`  グループホーム 生成エラー: ${e.message}`);
  }

  try {
    console.log(`[4/4] 放課後等デイサービス (サービス種類コード: 63 - 児童福祉法規格) 生成中...`);
    results.push(generateKokurenCsvForHouday(targetMonth));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error(`  放課後等デイ 生成エラー: ${e.message}`);
  }

  console.log(`\n================================================================================`);
  console.log(` 各サービス別 バリデーション結果サマリー`);
  console.log(`================================================================================`);

  let allPassed = true;

  for (const r of results) {
    const report = validateKokurenCsv(r);
    console.log(`\n【${report.serviceKey.toUpperCase()}型】サービスコード: ${report.serviceCode} (${report.facilityName})`);
    console.log(`  - 総レコード数: ${report.totalRecords} 件`);
    console.log(`  - 請求算定単位数: ${report.totalUnits.toLocaleString()} 単位`);
    console.log(`  - 給付費請求見込額: ${report.totalBillingAmount.toLocaleString()} 円`);
    console.log(`  - 仕様書適合判定: ${report.isValid ? '✅ 完全適合 (PASS)' : '❌ 不適合 (FAIL)'}`);

    if (report.errors.length > 0) {
      allPassed = false;
      console.log(`  - 検出エラー (${report.errors.length}件):`);
      report.errors.forEach(err => {
        console.log(`    * [行 ${err.lineIndex} / レコード ${err.recordType}] ${err.message}`);
      });
    }

    if (report.warnings.length > 0) {
      console.log(`  - 警告事項:`);
      report.warnings.forEach(w => console.log(`    * ${w}`));
    }
  }

  console.log(`\n================================================================================`);
  if (allPassed) {
    console.log(` 🎉 全4事業所の国保連伝送用CSVが、仕様書の全定義に「1ミリの誤差もなく」完全適合しました！`);
  } else {
    console.log(` ⚠️ 一部サービスで仕様不整合が検出されました。修正が必要です。`);
  }
  console.log(`================================================================================\n`);

  // 加算OFF時の誤請求ガード検証テスト
  const guardPassed = await testAdditionOffGuards(targetMonth);
  return allPassed && guardPassed;
}

/**
 * 加算設定OFF（未届出）時の誤請求防止ガード検証
 */
async function testAdditionOffGuards(targetMonth: string): Promise<boolean> {
  console.log(`================================================================================`);
  console.log(` 加算算定体制（ON/OFF制御） 誤請求防止ガード 厳格連動テスト`);
  console.log(`================================================================================`);

  let passed = true;

  // 1. A型: 欠席時対応加算(5871)・送迎加算(6500)をOFFにして検証
  try {
    const origA = getFacilityAdditions('type_a');
    saveFacilityAdditions('type_a', {
      absence_support: { isEnabled: false },
      transportation: { isEnabled: false, value: 'none' },
    });

    const csvA = generateKokurenCsvForTypeA(targetMonth);
    const hasAbsenceCode = csvA.csvText.includes(',43,5871,');
    const hasTransportCode = csvA.csvText.includes(',43,6500,');

    if (!hasAbsenceCode && !hasTransportCode) {
      console.log(`  [A型] 欠席時対応加算・送迎加算 OFF時除外テスト: ✅ 成功（未届出加算が明細から完全に除外）`);
    } else {
      passed = false;
      console.error(`  [A型] 欠席時対応加算・送迎加算 OFF時除外テスト: ❌ 失敗（OFFにもかかわらず明細に含まれています）`);
    }

    // 元の設定をリストア
    const restoreA: any = {};
    for (const item of origA) {
      restoreA[item.additionKey] = { isEnabled: item.isEnabled, value: item.value };
    }
    saveFacilityAdditions('type_a', restoreA);
  } catch (e: any) {
    passed = false;
    console.error(`  [A型] ガードテスト例外: ${e.message}`);
  }

  // 2. GH型: 夜間支援体制加算(6110)をOFFにして検証
  try {
    const origGh = getFacilityAdditions('gh');
    saveFacilityAdditions('gh', {
      night_support: { isEnabled: false, value: 'none' },
    });

    const csvGh = generateKokurenCsvForGh(targetMonth);
    const hasNightCode = csvGh.csvText.includes(',33,6110,');

    if (!hasNightCode) {
      console.log(`  [GH型] 夜間支援体制加算 OFF時除外テスト: ✅ 成功（未届出加算が明細から完全に除外）`);
    } else {
      passed = false;
      console.error(`  [GH型] 夜間支援体制加算 OFF時除外テスト: ❌ 失敗（OFFにもかかわらず明細に含まれています）`);
    }

    // リストア
    const restoreGh: any = {};
    for (const item of origGh) {
      restoreGh[item.additionKey] = { isEnabled: item.isEnabled, value: item.value };
    }
    saveFacilityAdditions('gh', restoreGh);
  } catch (e: any) {
    passed = false;
    console.error(`  [GH型] ガードテスト例外: ${e.message}`);
  }

  // 3. 放デイ型: 送迎加算(6200)をOFFにして検証
  try {
    const origHouday = getFacilityAdditions('houday');
    saveFacilityAdditions('houday', {
      transportation: { isEnabled: false, value: 'none' },
    });

    const csvHouday = generateKokurenCsvForHouday(targetMonth);
    const hasHoudayTransport = csvHouday.csvText.includes(',63,6200,');

    if (!hasHoudayTransport) {
      console.log(`  [放デイ型] 送迎加算 OFF時除外テスト: ✅ 成功（未届出加算が明細から完全に除外）`);
    } else {
      passed = false;
      console.error(`  [放デイ型] 送迎加算 OFF時除外テスト: ❌ 失敗（OFFにもかかわらず明細に含まれています）`);
    }

    // リストア
    const restoreHouday: any = {};
    for (const item of origHouday) {
      restoreHouday[item.additionKey] = { isEnabled: item.isEnabled, value: item.value };
    }
    saveFacilityAdditions('houday', restoreHouday);
  } catch (e: any) {
    passed = false;
    console.error(`  [放デイ型] ガードテスト例外: ${e.message}`);
  }

  console.log(`\n判定結果: ${passed ? '🎉 全サービスの未届出加算 誤請求防止ガードが100%確実に機能しています！' : '❌ ガードテスト失敗'}`);
  console.log(`================================================================================\n`);

  return passed;
}

// 直接スクリプト実行時
if (require.main === module) {
  runKokurenValidation().then(success => {
    process.exit(success ? 0 : 1);
  });
}

async function checkPages() {
  const pages = ['/', '/login', '/staff', '/clients', '/attendance', '/support-records', '/daily-logs', '/offsite', '/billing'];
  for (const p of pages) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('http://localhost:3000' + p, { headers: { 'x-role': 'admin' } });
      console.log(`Page: ${p} -> HTTP ${res.status}`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`Page: ${p} -> ERROR: ${e.message}`);
    }
  }
}
checkPages();

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import path from 'path';

const dbPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data.sqlite');
const db = new Database(dbPath);

console.log('🚨 利用者・職員・パート・全履歴・全月次データの完全消去を開始します...');

db.pragma('foreign_keys = OFF');

// 完全消去するテーブル一覧
const tablesToWipe = [
  // 利用者・職員
  'clients',
  'staff',
  'staff_attendance',
  'staff_shifts',
  'client_shifts',
  'employment_contracts',
  'health_records',

  // 日常記録・カルテ・日誌
  'attendance_records',
  'support_records',
  'daily_logs',

  // 給与・収支・月次データ
  'payslips',
  'monthly_payslips',
  'payroll_settings',
  'monthly_facility_units',
  'production_activities',
  'billing_monthly_locks',
  'monthly_provision_records',
  'monthly_burden_limits',
  'labor_agreements',

  // 個別支援計画・書類
  'support_plans',
  'plan_monitorings',
  'service_conferences',
  'assessments',
  'self_plans',
  'client_documents',

  // 各種記録・台帳
  'incident_reports',
  'complaint_records',
  'restraint_records',
  'committee_records',
  'training_records',
  'offsite_evaluations',
  'interview_records',
  'contractor_agreements',
  'offsite_workplaces',
  'backup_logs',
];

for (const tbl of tablesToWipe) {
  try {
    db.prepare(`DELETE FROM ${tbl}`).run();
    console.log(`  ✓ ${tbl} を完全に空にしました (0件)`);
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.warn(`  ! ${tbl}: ${e.message}`);
  }
}

db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

// データベースを真空圧縮 (VACUUM)
console.log('📦 データベースを真空圧縮 (VACUUM) しています...');
db.exec('VACUUM;');

console.log('\n--- 削除完了後の全テーブル状況 ---');
const tables = db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").all() as { name: string }[];
for (const t of tables) {
  const res = db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) as count FROM "${t.name}"`).get() as { count: number };
  console.log(`  ${t.name}: ${res.count} 件`);
}

console.log('\n✨ 完全リセット完了！ 利用者・職員・パート含め、全データが0件の完全白紙状態になりました。');

================================================================================
File: src/app/(corporate)/corporate/dashboard/page.tsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { getDbConnection } from '@/lib/db-client';
import { getGhDb } from '@/app/(gh)/gh/lib/db';
import { getHoudayDb } from '@/app/(houday)/houday/lib/db';
import {
  Building,
  TrendingUp,
  Users,
  DollarSign,
  Calendar,
  ArrowUpRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Sparkles
} from 'lucide-react';

import { getFacilityName, getCorporationName } from '@/lib/facilityHelper';

export const metadata = {
  title: '全事業所 統合経営ダッシュボード | 法人統括本部',
};

export default function CorporateDashboardPage() {
  const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);

  // 1. 就労A型データ集計（local_data.sqlite）
  const dbA = getDbConnection('type_a');
  let aClients = 0;
  let aCapacity = 20;
  let aKokuhoEstimate = 0;
  try {
    const row = dbA.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM clients WHERE status = 'active' AND deleted_at IS NULL").get() as any;
    aClients = row ? row.cnt : 0;
    const setRow = dbA.prepare("SELECT capacity_class FROM facility_settings LIMIT 1").get() as any;
    if (setRow && setRow.capacity_class) {
      aCapacity = Number(setRow.capacity_class) || 20;
    }
    aKokuhoEstimate = Math.round(aClients * 185000);
  } catch (e) {
    aClients = 0;
  }
  const aOccupancy = aCapacity > 0 ? Math.round((aClients / aCapacity) * 100) : 0;

  // 2. 就労B型データ集計（local_data_b.sqlite）
  const dbB = getDbConnection('type_b');
  let bClients = 0;
  let bCapacity = 20;
  let bAvgWage = 18500;
  let bKokuhoEstimate = 0;
  try {
    const row = dbB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM clients WHERE status = 'active' AND deleted_at IS NULL").get() as any;
    bClients = row ? row.cnt : 0;
    const setRow = dbB.prepare("SELECT capacity_class FROM facility_settings LIMIT 1").get() as any;
    if (setRow && setRow.capacity_class) {
      bCapacity = Number(setRow.capacity_class) || 20;
    }
    const wageRow = dbB.prepare("SELECT AVG(total_wage) as avg_wage FROM monthly_payslips WHERE target_month = ?").get(currentMonth) as any;
    if (wageRow && wageRow.avg_wage) {
      bAvgWage = Math.round(wageRow.avg_wage);
    }
    bKokuhoEstimate = Math.round(bClients * 145000);
  } catch (e) {
    bClients = 0;
  }
  const bOccupancy = bCapacity > 0 ? Math.round((bClients / bCapacity) * 100) : 0;

  // 3. グループホームデータ集計（local_data_gh.sqlite）
  const dbGh = getGhDb();
  let ghResidents = 0;
  let ghCapacity = 8;
  let ghActualRevenue = 0;
  let ghKokuhoEstimate = 0;
  try {
    const resCount = dbGh.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_residents WHERE status = 'active'").get() as any;
    ghResidents = resCount ? resCount.cnt : 0;
    const setRow = dbGh.prepare("SELECT capacity FROM gh_settings LIMIT 1").get() as any;
    if (setRow && setRow.capacity) {
      ghCapacity = Number(setRow.capacity) || 8;
    }
    const billSum = dbGh.prepare("SELECT SUM(total_resident_payment) as sum FROM gh_monthly_billings WHERE target_month = ?").get(currentMonth) as any;
    ghActualRevenue = billSum && billSum.sum ? billSum.sum : 0;
    ghKokuhoEstimate = Math.round(ghResidents * 180000);
  } catch (e) {
    ghResidents = 0;
    ghActualRevenue = 0;
  }
  const ghOccupancy = ghCapacity > 0 ? Math.round((ghResidents / ghCapacity) * 100) : 0;

  // 4. 放課後等デイデータ集計（local_data_houday.sqlite）
  const dbHouday = getHoudayDb();
  let houdayChildren = 0;
  let houdayCapacity = 10;
  let houdayActualRevenue = 0;
  let houdayKokuhoEstimate = 0;
  try {
    const childCount = dbHouday.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM houday_children WHERE status = 'active'").get() as any;
    houdayChildren = childCount ? childCount.cnt : 0;
    const setRow = dbHouday.prepare("SELECT capacity FROM houday_settings LIMIT 1").get() as any;
    if (setRow && setRow.capacity) {
      houdayCapacity = Number(setRow.capacity) || 10;
    }
    const billSum = dbHouday.prepare("SELECT SUM(guardian_actual_cost) as sum, SUM(kokuho_units) as units FROM houday_monthly_billings WHERE target_month = ?").get(currentMonth) as any;
    houdayActualRevenue = billSum && billSum.sum ? billSum.sum : 0;
    if (billSum && billSum.units) {
      houdayKokuhoEstimate = Math.round(billSum.units * 10.66);
    } else {
      houdayKokuhoEstimate = Math.round(houdayChildren * 190000);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    houdayChildren = 0;
    houdayActualRevenue = 0;
  }
  const houdayOccupancy = houdayCapacity > 0 ? Math.round((houdayChildren / houdayCapacity) * 100) : 0;

  // 法人全体合算
  const totalClients = aClients + bClients + ghResidents + houdayChildren;
  const totalCapacity = aCapacity + bCapacity + ghCapacity + houdayCapacity;
  const totalOccupancy = totalCapacity > 0 ? Math.round((totalClients / totalCapacity) * 100) : 0;

  const totalKokuhoRevenue = aKokuhoEstimate + bKokuhoEstimate + ghKokuhoEstimate + houdayKokuhoEstimate;
  const totalActualRevenue = ghActualRevenue + houdayActualRevenue;
  const grandTotalRevenue = totalKokuhoRevenue + totalActualRevenue;

  const aFacilityName = getFacilityName('type_a');
  const bFacilityName = getFacilityName('type_b');
  const ghFacilityName = getFacilityName('gh');
  const houdayFacilityName = getFacilityName('houday');
  const corpName = getCorporationName();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {/* 画面ヘッダー */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Building className="w-7 h-7 text-purple-600" />
            {corpName} 全事業所 運営統括ダッシュボード
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            対象月: {currentMonth} ｜ 就労A型・就労B型・グループホーム・放課後等デイサービスの定員稼働・給付費収入見込
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 flex items-center gap-3">
          <Calendar className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600" />
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">給付費 次回支払予定日（国保連）</div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">翌月15日（約 {Math.round(totalKokuhoRevenue * 0.9).toLocaleString()}円）</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 法人全体サマリーカード */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 to-purple-950 text-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-md border border-purple-900">
          <div className="text-xs text-purple-300 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">法人総定員・現員</div>
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline gap-2">
            <span className="text-3xl font-black">{totalClients}</span>
            <span className="text-sm text-purple-300">/ 定員 {totalCapacity}名</span>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-purple-200 flex items-center gap-1">
            <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            法人総合稼働率 <span className="font-bold text-white ml-1">{totalOccupancy}%</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">月間収入見込（給付費 ＋ 実費）</div>
          <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">
            {grandTotalRevenue.toLocaleString()}円
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-emerald-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            国保連給付費 ＋ 実費徴収
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">国保連給付費 請求見込</div>
          <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-black text-purple-700">
            {totalKokuhoRevenue.toLocaleString()}円
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
            4事業所合算給付費
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">利用者・保護者実費徴収額</div>
          <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-black text-amber-700">
            {totalActualRevenue.toLocaleString()}円
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
            家賃・食費・水光熱・おやつ代
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4事業所別 統合ステータスカード */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* 1. 就労A型 */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 p-6 space-y-4 hover:border-indigo-300 transition-all">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="p-2.5 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg">
                🏢
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-800">{aFacilityName}</h3>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">雇用型 ｜ 労基法 ｜ 最低賃金 ｜ スコア方式</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/a/dashboard"
              className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-800 flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              詳細画面 <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-center">
            <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-bold">利用人数</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-black text-slate-800 mt-1">{aClients}名</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">定員 {aCapacity}名</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-bold">稼働率</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 mt-1">{aOccupancy}%</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 font-bold">定員充足率: {aOccupancy}%</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-bold">給付費見込</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-black text-slate-800 mt-1">{(aKokuhoEstimate / 10000).toFixed(1)}万円</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">スコア判定連動</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg">
                🌱
              </span>

                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-800">{bFacilityName}</h3>

              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-bold">平均工賃月額</div>
