import { getDbConnection } from '@/lib/db-client';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

let isGhDbInitialized = false;

export function getGhDb(): Database.Database {
  const db = getDbConnection('gh');
  if (!isGhDbInitialized) {
    initGhDatabase(db);
    isGhDbInitialized = true;
  }
  return db;
}

function initGhDatabase(db: Database.Database) {
  db.exec(`
    -- 1. GH事業所・実費設定
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_settings (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      facility_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      capacity INTEGER DEFAULT 10,
      standard_rent INTEGER DEFAULT 35000,
      special_benefit_deduction INTEGER DEFAULT 10000, -- 特定障害者特別給付費（市町村家賃補助）
      breakfast_price INTEGER DEFAULT 300,
      lunch_price INTEGER DEFAULT 450,
      dinner_price INTEGER DEFAULT 550,
      utilities_cost INTEGER DEFAULT 12000,           -- 水光熱費
      daily_supplies_cost INTEGER DEFAULT 3000,       -- 日用品費
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );

    -- 2. ユニット・居室管理
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_units (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      unit_name TEXT NOT NULL,       -- 例: '本館 1号館', 'サテライト荘'
      room_number TEXT NOT NULL,     -- 例: '101', '102'
      floor INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
      capacity INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
      is_occupied INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- 0: 空室, 1: 入居中
      notes TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );

    -- 3. GH入居者管理（障害支援区分・居室紐付け）
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_residents (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      furigana TEXT,
      recipient_number TEXT,
      support_level INTEGER DEFAULT 3, -- 障害支援区分: 1〜6 (自立/区分なしは0)
      unit_id TEXT,
      room_number TEXT,
      rent_amount INTEGER DEFAULT 35000,
      receives_special_benefit INTEGER DEFAULT 1, -- 特定障害者特別給付費（1万円控除対象）
      service_start_date TEXT,
      service_end_date TEXT,
      status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',               -- 'active', 'hospitalized', 'left'
      guardian_name TEXT,
      guardian_contact TEXT,
      emergency_contact TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES gh_units(id)
    );

    -- 4. 外泊トラッカー（帰省・入院・食事連動）
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_overnight_stays (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      stay_type TEXT DEFAULT 'home', -- 'home'(帰省), 'hospital'(入院), 'other'(外泊)
      start_date TEXT NOT NULL,      -- 開始日
      end_date TEXT,                 -- 終了日
      return_addition_eligible INTEGER DEFAULT 1, -- 帰宅時加算対象（月6日以内）
      deduct_breakfast INTEGER DEFAULT 1,         -- 朝食代0円連動
      deduct_dinner INTEGER DEFAULT 1,            -- 夕食代0円連動
      reason TEXT,
      notes TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (resident_id) REFERENCES gh_residents(id)
    );

    -- 5. 夜間巡回・生活記録・バイタル
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_daily_logs (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      log_date TEXT NOT NULL,
      staff_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      shift_type TEXT DEFAULT 'night', -- 'night'(夜勤・宿直), 'day'(日勤・世話人)
      patrol_22 TEXT,                  -- 22:00 巡回状況
      patrol_01 TEXT,                  -- 01:00 巡回状況
      patrol_04 TEXT,                  -- 04:00 巡回状況
      general_notes TEXT,
      incident_notes TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_vital_and_meds (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      record_date TEXT NOT NULL,
      temperature REAL,
      blood_pressure TEXT,             -- 例: '120/80'
      pulse INTEGER,
      med_morning INTEGER DEFAULT 1,   -- 朝服薬 (1:完了, 0:未/不要)
      med_noon INTEGER DEFAULT 0,      -- 昼服薬
      med_evening INTEGER DEFAULT 1,   -- 夕服薬
      med_night INTEGER DEFAULT 1,     -- 就寝前服薬
      meal_breakfast INTEGER DEFAULT 1,-- 朝食実食 (1:食べた, 0:欠食)
      meal_lunch INTEGER DEFAULT 0,    -- 昼食実食
      meal_dinner INTEGER DEFAULT 1,   -- 夕食実食
      notes TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (resident_id) REFERENCES gh_residents(id),
      UNIQUE(resident_id, record_date)
    );

    -- 6. 預かり金出納帳（お小遣い・レシート管理）
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_resident_money_ledgers (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      transaction_date TEXT NOT NULL,
      transaction_type TEXT NOT NULL,   -- 'income'(預かり/年金), 'expense'(支出/買物)
      category TEXT NOT NULL,           -- 'お小遣い', '年金受給', '医療費', '日用品', '理美容', 'レシート精算'
      amount INTEGER NOT NULL,
      balance_after INTEGER NOT NULL,
      description TEXT,
      receipt_number TEXT,
      checked_by TEXT,                  -- 確認者（世話人・管理者）
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (resident_id) REFERENCES gh_residents(id)
    );

    -- 7. 実費精算＆個人請求書
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_monthly_billings (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      target_month TEXT NOT NULL,       -- YYYY-MM
      stay_days INTEGER DEFAULT 30,     -- 入居日数
      rent_base INTEGER DEFAULT 35000,
      rent_subsidy_deduction INTEGER DEFAULT 10000, -- 特定給付費控除
      rent_net INTEGER DEFAULT 25000,   -- 差引家賃個人負担
      breakfast_count INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
      lunch_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      dinner_count INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
      meal_total INTEGER DEFAULT 25500,
      utilities_cost INTEGER DEFAULT 12000,
      supplies_cost INTEGER DEFAULT 3000,
      other_expenses INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      total_resident_payment INTEGER NOT NULL, -- 個人請求総額
      kokuho_service_units INTEGER DEFAULT 0,  -- 国保連給付費算定単位数
      is_locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      FOREIGN KEY (resident_id) REFERENCES gh_residents(id),
      UNIQUE(resident_id, target_month)
    );

    -- 8. 事故・ヒヤリハット記録（管理者最終決裁）
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_incidents (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT,
      occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      incident_type TEXT NOT NULL, -- 'accident', 'near_miss'
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      description TEXT NOT NULL,
      severity TEXT DEFAULT 'low',
      status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending_approval', -- 'draft', 'pending_approval', 'approved'
      reporter_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      approver_name TEXT,
      approved_at TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );

    -- 9. 日中活動先連携・会議録（サビ管）
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS gh_coordination_logs (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      resident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      target_date TEXT NOT NULL,
      organization_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      coordinator_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      purpose TEXT NOT NULL,
      content TEXT NOT NULL,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );

    -- インデックス
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_gh_residents_unit ON gh_residents(unit_id);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_gh_overnight_resident ON gh_overnight_stays(resident_id, start_date);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_gh_vital_resident_date ON gh_vital_and_meds(resident_id, record_date);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_gh_money_resident_date ON gh_resident_money_ledgers(resident_id, transaction_date);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_gh_billings_month ON gh_monthly_billings(resident_id, target_month);
  `);

  // カラム追加マイグレーション
  const addColumns = [
    { table: 'gh_residents', col: 'plan_start_date TEXT' },
    { table: 'gh_residents', col: 'plan_end_date TEXT' },
    { table: 'gh_residents', col: 'monitoring_due_date TEXT' },
    { table: 'gh_residents', col: 'certificate_expiry TEXT' },
    { table: 'gh_residents', col: 'daytime_activity TEXT' },
    { table: 'gh_settings', col: 'caretaker_fte_ratio REAL DEFAULT 4.2' },
    { table: 'gh_settings', col: 'night_staff_count INTEGER DEFAULT 1' },
    { table: 'gh_settings', col: 'waiting_count INTEGER DEFAULT 3' },
  ];
  for (const { table, col } of addColumns) {
    try {
      db.exec(`ALTER TABLE ${table} ADD COLUMN ${col}`);
    } catch (e) {
      // 既に存在する場合はスキップ
    }
  }

  // 初期シードデータ投入（未投入時のみ）
  const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_settings').get() as any;
  if (count.cnt === 0) {
    seedGhInitialData(db);
  } else {
    // 既存レコードの補完更新
    try {
      db.exec(`
        UPDATE gh_residents SET 
          plan_start_date = COALESCE(plan_start_date, '2026-05-01'),
          plan_end_date = COALESCE(plan_end_date, '2026-10-31'),
          monitoring_due_date = COALESCE(monitoring_due_date, '2026-10-25'),
          certificate_expiry = COALESCE(certificate_expiry, '2027-03-31'),
          daytime_activity = COALESCE(daytime_activity, '就労継続支援A型（かがやきワーク）')
        WHERE plan_end_date IS NULL;
        UPDATE gh_residents SET plan_end_date = '2026-10-20', monitoring_due_date = '2026-10-18' WHERE id = 'res_001';
        UPDATE gh_residents SET certificate_expiry = '2026-11-15', daytime_activity = '就労継続支援B型（かがやきファクトリー）' WHERE id = 'res_002';
        UPDATE gh_residents SET daytime_activity = '生活介護（あおぞらデイ）' WHERE id = 'res_003';
      `);
      const incCount = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_incidents').get() as any).cnt;
      if (incCount === 0) {
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO gh_incidents (id, resident_id, occurred_at, incident_type, title, description, severity, status, reporter_name)
          VALUES ('inc_001', 'res_001', '2026-10-10 19:30', 'near_miss', '洗面台での足元のふらつき', '夕食後に居室へ戻る際、スリッパが滑りバランスを崩しかけた。怪我なし。滑り止めマットを設置対応。', 'low', 'pending_approval', '世話人 佐々木')
        `).run();
      }
      const coordCount = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_coordination_logs').get() as any).cnt;
      if (coordCount === 0) {
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO gh_coordination_logs (id, resident_id, target_date, organization_name, coordinator_name, purpose, content)
          VALUES ('coord_001', 'res_001', '2026-10-08', '就労継続支援A型 かがやきワーク', 'サビ管 山本', '日中作業状況と生活リズムの共有', 'A型での軽作業の集中力は良好。夜間の睡眠状況が作業効率に直結するため、消灯時刻（22:30）の遵守をホーム側で促すことを申し合わせた。')
        `).run();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // 補完失敗は無視
    }
  }
}

function seedGhInitialData(db: Database.Database) {
  db.transaction(() => {
    // 設定
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO gh_settings (id, facility_name, capacity, standard_rent, special_benefit_deduction, breakfast_price, lunch_price, dinner_price, utilities_cost, daily_supplies_cost)
      VALUES ('gh_default', 'グループホーム かがやき荘（共同生活援助）', 8, 35000, 10000, 300, 450, 550, 12000, 3000)
    `).run();

    // ユニット・居室（8部屋）
    const rooms = [
      { id: 'u101', name: '本館 1号館', room: '101', floor: 1, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u102', name: '本館 1号館', room: '102', floor: 1, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u103', name: '本館 1号館', room: '103', floor: 1, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u104', name: '本館 1号館', room: '104', floor: 1, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u201', name: '本館 2号館', room: '201', floor: 2, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u202', name: '本館 2号館', room: '202', floor: 2, occ: 1 },
      { id: 'u203', name: '本館 2号館', room: '203', floor: 2, occ: 0 }, // 空室
      { id: 'u204', name: '本館 2号館', room: '204', floor: 2, occ: 0 }, // 空室
    ];
    const insRoom = db.prepare('INSERT INTO gh_units (id, unit_name, room_number, floor, capacity, is_occupied) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)');
    for (const r of rooms) {
      insRoom.run(r.id, r.name, r.room, r.floor, 1, r.occ);
    }

    // 入居者（6名）
    const residents = [
      { id: 'res_001', name: '佐藤 健一', furi: 'サトウ ケンイチ', rec: '1234567891', level: 4, uid: 'u101', room: '101', rent: 35000, sub: 1, start: '2023-04-01' },
      { id: 'res_002', name: '高橋 優子', furi: 'タカハシ ユウコ', rec: '1234567892', level: 3, uid: 'u102', room: '102', rent: 35000, sub: 1, start: '2023-06-15' },
      { id: 'res_003', name: '鈴木 雅也', furi: 'スズキ マサヤ', rec: '1234567893', level: 5, uid: 'u103', room: '103', rent: 35000, sub: 1, start: '2022-10-01' },
      { id: 'res_004', name: '伊藤 美咲', furi: 'イトウ ミサキ', rec: '1234567894', level: 2, uid: 'u104', room: '104', rent: 35000, sub: 1, start: '2024-01-10' },
      { id: 'res_005', name: '渡辺 翔太', furi: 'ワタナベ ショウタ', rec: '1234567895', level: 4, uid: 'u201', room: '201', rent: 35000, sub: 1, start: '2024-04-01' },
      { id: 'res_006', name: '小林 陽子', furi: 'コバヤシ ヨウコ', rec: '1234567896', level: 3, uid: 'u202', room: '202', rent: 35000, sub: 0, start: '2024-05-01' }, // 給付費対象外
    ];
    const insRes = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO gh_residents (id, name, furigana, recipient_number, support_level, unit_id, room_number, rent_amount, receives_special_benefit, service_start_date)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);
    for (const res of residents) {
      insRes.run(res.id, res.name, res.furi, res.rec, res.level, res.uid, res.room, res.rent, res.sub, res.start);
    }

    // 預かり金出納帳の初期残高
    const insMoney = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO gh_resident_money_ledgers (id, resident_id, transaction_date, transaction_type, category, amount, balance_after, description, checked_by)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);
    insMoney.run('m_001', 'res_001', '2026-10-01', 'income', '年金受給', 65000, 65000, '10月期 障害年金入金預かり', '世話人 佐々木');
    insMoney.run('m_002', 'res_001', '2026-10-05', 'expense', 'お小遣い', 5000, 60000, '本人希望による週間小遣い出金', '世話人 佐々木');
    insMoney.run('m_003', 'res_001', '2026-10-08', 'expense', '日用品', 2180, 57820, 'シャンプー・歯磨き粉購入（レシートNo.104）', '世話人 佐々木');

    // 生活記録・バイタル
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const insVital = db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO gh_vital_and_meds (id, resident_id, record_date, temperature, blood_pressure, pulse, med_morning, med_evening, med_night, meal_breakfast, meal_dinner)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);
    insVital.run('v_001', 'res_001', today, 36.5, '118/76', 72, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1);
    insVital.run('v_002', 'res_002', today, 36.8, '124/82', 78, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1);
    insVital.run('v_003', 'res_003', today, 36.4, '130/85', 70, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1);

    // 月次請求書（当月見込み）
    const targetMonth = today.slice(0, 7);
    const insBill = db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO gh_monthly_billings (
        id, resident_id, target_month, stay_days, rent_base, rent_subsidy_deduction, rent_net,
        breakfast_count, lunch_count, dinner_count, meal_total, utilities_cost, supplies_cost, total_resident_payment
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);
    for (const res of residents) {
      const netRent = res.sub ? Math.max(0, res.rent - 10000) : res.rent;
      const meal = (28 * 300) + (28 * 550); // 23,800円
      const total = netRent + meal + 12000 + 3000;
      insBill.run(`bill_${res.id}_${targetMonth}`, res.id, targetMonth, 28, res.rent, res.sub ? 10000 : 0, netRent, 28, 0, 28, meal, 12000, 3000, total);
    }
  })();
}

'use server';

import { getGhDb } from '../lib/db';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';

export async function addMoneyTransaction(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getGhDb();
    const id = `money_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    const residentId = formData.get('residentId') as string;
    const transactionDate = formData.get('transactionDate') as string || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const transactionType = formData.get('transactionType') as string; // 'income' | 'expense'
    const category = formData.get('category') as string;
    const amount = parseInt(formData.get('amount') as string) || 0;
    const description = formData.get('description') as string || '';
    const receiptNumber = formData.get('receiptNumber') as string || null;
    const checkedBy = formData.get('checkedBy') as string || '世話人';

    if (amount <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    db.transaction(() => {
      // 最新残高を取得
      const lastTx = db.prepare(`
        SELECT balance_after FROM gh_resident_money_ledgers
        WHERE resident_id = ?
        ORDER BY transaction_date DESC, created_at DESC
        LIMIT 1
      `).get(residentId) as any;

      const currentBalance = lastTx ? lastTx.balance_after : 0;
      const newBalance = transactionType === 'income' ? currentBalance + amount : currentBalance - amount;

      if (newBalance < 0 && transactionType === 'expense') {
        throw new Error(`残高不足です（現在の預かり金残高: ${currentBalance.toLocaleString()}円）`);
      }

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO gh_resident_money_ledgers (
          id, resident_id, transaction_date, transaction_type, category, amount, balance_after, description, receipt_number, checked_by
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(id, residentId, transactionDate, transactionType, category, amount, newBalance, description, receiptNumber, checkedBy);
    })();

    revalidatePath('/gh/money');
    revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('addMoneyTransaction error:', e);
  }
}

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

import { Wallet, Plus, Printer, ArrowDownRight, ArrowUpRight, ShieldCheck, FileSpreadsheet } from "lucide-react";
import { PrintButton } from "@/components/ui/PrintButton";
import { getGhDb } from '../lib/db';
import { addMoneyTransaction } from './actions';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function GhMoneyPage(props: { searchParams?: Promise<{ residentId?: string }> }) {
  const searchParams = await props.searchParams;
  const db = getGhDb();

  const residents = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM gh_residents WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY room_number ASC").all() as any[];
  const selectedResidentId = searchParams?.residentId || (residents[0]?.id || '');
  const selectedResident = residents.find(r => r.id === selectedResidentId) || residents[0];

  // 全入居者の最新残高サマリー
  const balanceSummary = residents.map(r => {
    const last = db.prepare(`
      SELECT balance_after FROM gh_resident_money_ledgers
      WHERE resident_id = ?
      ORDER BY transaction_date DESC, created_at DESC
      LIMIT 1
    `).get(r.id) as any;
    return {
      resident: r,
      balance: last ? last.balance_after : 0
    };
  });

  // 選択入居者の出納明細
  const ledgers = selectedResident ? db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM gh_resident_money_ledgers
    WHERE resident_id = ?
    ORDER BY transaction_date DESC, created_at DESC
  `).all(selectedResident.id) as any[] : [];

  const currentBalance = ledgers[0]?.balance_after ?? 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-amber-950 tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="w-7 h-7 text-amber-600" />
            預かり金出納帳（お小遣い・年金・レシート管理）
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-amber-800/90 mt-0.5">
            共同生活援助における入居者個人の金銭預託台帳。実地指導時の金銭管理監査基準に完全対応
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* 監査提出用 印刷ボタン */}
          <PrintButton 
            label="台帳を印刷（監査提出用）"
            className="border-amber-300 text-amber-900 bg-white hover:bg-amber-100 font-bold text-xs h-9 shadow-xs"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 入居者別 残高クイックピッカー */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
        {balanceSummary.map(item => (
          <Link
            key={item.resident.id}
            href={`/gh/money?residentId=${item.resident.id}`}
            className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
              item.resident.id === selectedResidentId
                ? 'bg-amber-100/80 border-amber-500 ring-2 ring-amber-400/40 shadow-xs'
                : 'bg-white border-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-50/50'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-amber-900">
              {item.resident.room_number}号室
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900 truncate">
              {item.resident.name} 様
            </div>
            <div className="font-mono font-black text-sm text-amber-950 mt-1">
              {item.balance.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-[10px] font-normal">円</span>
            </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>

      {selectedResident && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {/* 左側2カラム: 出納明細一覧 */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
            <Card className="border-amber-200 shadow-2xs">
              <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-amber-100 flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <CardTitle className="text-base font-bold text-amber-950">
                      {selectedResident.name} 様 出納明細台帳
                    </CardTitle>
                    <Badge className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-300 font-mono text-xs">
                      {selectedResident.room_number}号室
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                  <CardDescription className="text-xs">
                    受給者証番号: {selectedResident.recipient_number || '-'}
                  </CardDescription>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground font-medium">現在残高</div>
                  <div className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-950 font-mono">
                    {currentBalance.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs font-normal">円</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="p-0">
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="w-full text-xs text-left">
                    <thead className="bg-amber-50/70 border-b border-amber-200 text-amber-900 font-bold">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="p-2.5">取引日</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5">区分</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5">費目</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5">摘要・購入明細</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5 text-right">入金額</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5 text-right">出金額</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5 text-right">差引残高</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5">レシートNo</th>
                        <th className="p-2.5">確認印</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-amber-100">
                      {ledgers.map(l => (
                        <tr key={l.id} className="hover:bg-amber-50/40">
                          <td className="p-2.5 font-mono">{l.transaction_date}</td>
                          <td className="p-2.5">
                            {l.transaction_type === 'income' ? (
                              <Badge className="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 text-[10px]">
                                預かり入金
                              </Badge>
                            ) : (
                              <Badge className="bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border-rose-300 text-[10px]">
                                支出
                              </Badge>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 font-bold">{l.category}</td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 max-w-xs truncate text-muted-foreground">{l.description}</td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 text-right font-mono font-bold text-emerald-700">
                            {l.transaction_type === 'income' ? `+${l.amount.toLocaleString()}円` : '-'}
                          </td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 text-right font-mono font-bold text-rose-700">
                            {l.transaction_type === 'expense' ? `-${l.amount.toLocaleString()}円` : '-'}
                          </td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 text-right font-mono font-black text-amber-950">
                            {l.balance_after.toLocaleString()}円
                          </td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500">{l.receipt_number || '-'}</td>
                          <td className="p-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-600">{l.checked_by || '世話人'}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                      {ledgers.length === 0 && (
                        <tr>
                          <td colSpan={9} className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                            出納記録がありません。右のフォームから入出金を登録してください。
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      )}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>

                {/* 監査提出用 月次照合・承認印欄 */}
                <div className="p-4 border-t border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/30 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="space-y-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                    <div>※ 本台帳は各取引レシート原本と照合の上、入居者預かり金管理規定に基づき保管・監査を行っています。</div>
                    <div>残高実査確認日: 令和　年　月　日（帳簿残高と現金・通帳残高の一致を確認）</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-4 text-center shrink-0">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-600 mb-0.5">管理者</div>
                      <div className="w-12 h-12 border border-slate-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] text-slate-400 bg-white">
                        印
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-600 mb-0.5">サビ管</div>
                      <div className="w-12 h-12 border border-slate-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] text-slate-400 bg-white">
                        印

                      <div className="w-12 h-12 border border-slate-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] text-slate-400 bg-white">

                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>

          </div>

            <Card className="border-amber-200 shadow-2xs">

                </CardTitle>
                <CardDescription className="text-xs">
                  {selectedResident.name} 様の口座へ出納を登録します
                </CardDescription>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="pt-4">

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-bold">取引区分 *</Label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">

                        出金 (お小遣い/買物)

                        <input type="radio" name="transactionType" value="income" />
                        入金 (年金/家族預かり)
                      </label>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <Label className="text-xs font-bold">取引日 *</Label>
                      <Input name="transactionDate" type="date" required defaultValue={new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)} className="h-8 text-xs font-mono" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <Label className="text-xs font-bold">費目 *</Label>
                      <select name="category" required className="w-full h-8 text-xs border rounded-md px-2 bg-background font-bold">
                        <option value="お小遣い">お小遣い</option>
                        <option value="日用品">日用品購入</option>
                        <option value="医療費">医療費・薬代</option>
                        <option value="理美容">理美容費</option>
                        <option value="年金受給">年金受給預かり</option>
                        <option value="家族預かり">ご家族からの預託</option>
                        <option value="その他">その他</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-bold">金額 (円) *</Label>
                    <Input name="amount" type="number" required placeholder="例: 3000" min="1" className="h-8 text-xs font-mono text-base font-bold" />
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-bold">摘要・購入内容</Label>
                    <Input name="description" placeholder="例: 週末用のお小遣い、歯ブラシ等購入" className="h-8 text-xs" />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <Label className="text-xs font-bold">レシートNo / 領収書番号</Label>
                      <Input name="receiptNumber" placeholder="例: No.105" className="h-8 text-xs font-mono" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <Label className="text-xs font-bold">確認職員名</Label>
                      <Input name="checkedBy" defaultValue="世話人 佐々木" className="h-8 text-xs" />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <Button type="submit" className="w-full bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-bold h-8 text-xs mt-2">
                    出納を登録する
                  </Button>
                </form>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            {/* 監査基準アラート */}
            <div className="p-3 bg-amber-100/60 border border-amber-300 rounded-lg text-xs text-amber-950 space-y-1 leading-relaxed">
              <div className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-900">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700" />
                実地指導（監査）の金銭管理留意点
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px]">
                出納記録は必ず領収書またはレシートと突き合わせて保存してください。月末には残高と現金実査を行い、台帳へ確認印（サイン）を付すことが求められます。
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import React, { useTransition } from 'react';
import { Trash2, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { deleteResident } from './actions';

interface DeleteGhResidentButtonProps {
  residentId: string;
  residentName: string;
  roomNumber?: string;
}

export function DeleteGhResidentButton({
  residentId,
  residentName,
  roomNumber,
}: DeleteGhResidentButtonProps) {
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleDelete = () => {
    const roomText = roomNumber ? `（${roomNumber}号室）` : '';
    const ok = window.confirm(
      `【確認】入居者「${residentName} 様」${roomText}の台帳を削除しますか？\n※割り当てられていた居室は自動的に空室に戻ります。`
    );
    if (!ok) return;

    startTransition(async () => {
      const res = await deleteResident(residentId);
      if (res && !res.success) {
        alert(res.error || '入居者の削除に失敗しました');
      }
    });
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleDelete}
      disabled={isPending}
      title="入居者を削除・退去"
      className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition-colors border border-transparent hover:border-rose-200 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {isPending ? (
        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-rose-600" />
      ) : (
        <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
      )}
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
File: src/app/(gh)/gh/residents/actions.ts

'use server';

import { getGhDb } from '../lib/db';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';

export async function addResident(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getGhDb();
    const id = `res_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    const name = formData.get('name') as string;
    const furigana = formData.get('furigana') as string;
    const recipientNumber = formData.get('recipientNumber') as string;
    const supportLevel = parseInt(formData.get('supportLevel') as string) || 3;
    const unitId = formData.get('unitId') as string;
    const roomNumber = formData.get('roomNumber') as string;
    const rentAmount = parseInt(formData.get('rentAmount') as string) || 35000;
    const receivesSpecialBenefit = formData.get('receivesSpecialBenefit') === '1' ? 1 : 0;
    const serviceStartDate = formData.get('serviceStartDate') as string || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    db.transaction(() => {
      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO gh_residents (
          id, name, furigana, recipient_number, support_level, unit_id, room_number,
          rent_amount, receives_special_benefit, service_start_date, status
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active')
      `).run(id, name, furigana, recipientNumber, supportLevel, unitId, roomNumber, rentAmount, receivesSpecialBenefit, serviceStartDate);

      if (unitId) {
        db.prepare('UPDATE gh_units SET is_occupied = 1 WHERE id = ?').run(unitId);
      }
    })();

    revalidatePath('/gh/residents');
    revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('addResident error:', e);
  }
}

export async function addOvernightStay(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getGhDb();
    const id = `stay_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    const residentId = formData.get('residentId') as string;
    const stayType = formData.get('stayType') as string || 'home';
    const startDate = formData.get('startDate') as string;
    const endDate = formData.get('endDate') as string || null;
    const reason = formData.get('reason') as string || '';

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO gh_overnight_stays (
        id, resident_id, stay_type, start_date, end_date, return_addition_eligible, deduct_breakfast, deduct_dinner, reason
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, 1, ?)
    `).run(id, residentId, stayType, startDate, endDate, reason);

    revalidatePath('/gh/residents');
    revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('addOvernightStay error:', e);
  }
}

export async function deleteResident(residentId: string) {
  try {
    if (!residentId) {
      return { success: false, error: '入居者IDが指定されていません' };
    }
    const db = getGhDb();

    // 割当居室IDの取得
    const resident = db.prepare('SELECT unit_id FROM gh_residents WHERE id = ?').get(residentId) as any;
    const unitId = resident?.unit_id;

    db.transaction(() => {
      // 関連データの削除（子テーブル全件）
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_overnight_stays WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_vital_and_meds WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_resident_money_ledgers WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_monthly_billings WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      try {
        db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_daily_records WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      } catch (e) {}
      try {
        db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_coordination_logs WHERE resident_id = ?').run(residentId);
      } catch (e) {}

      // 入居者本体の削除
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_residents WHERE id = ?').run(residentId);

      // 居室の空室化（該当居室に他の入居者がいなければ is_occupied = 0 に戻す）
      if (unitId) {
        const remaining = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_residents WHERE unit_id = ? AND status = 'active'").get(unitId) as any;
        if (!remaining || remaining.cnt === 0) {
          db.prepare('UPDATE gh_units SET is_occupied = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(unitId);
        }
      }
    })();

    try {
      revalidatePath('/gh/residents');
      revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
      revalidatePath('/gh/settings');
    } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true };
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('deleteResident error:', e);
    return { success: false, error: e?.message || '入居者の削除に失敗しました' };
  }
}

              </div>

              <div className="p-2 rounded bg-blue-50/80 border border-blue-200 text-[11px] text-blue-900 leading-tight">
                💡 外泊期間中は朝食代（300円/回）・夕食代（550円/回）が月末の請求書で自動的に0円として控除されます。
              </div>

              <Button type="submit" className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold h-8 text-xs mt-2">
                外泊・帰省を登録する
              </Button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
File: src/app/(gh)/gh/residents/provision-records/page.tsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { getGhDb } from '../../lib/db';
import { PrintButton } from '@/components/ui/PrintButton';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { ArrowLeft, Printer, Users } from 'lucide-react';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function GhProvisionRecordsPage(props: {
  searchParams?: Promise<{ month?: string; residentId?: string }>;
}) {
  const searchParams = await props.searchParams;
  const currentMonthStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
  const targetMonth = searchParams?.month || currentMonthStr;
  const db = getGhDb();

  const settings = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM gh_settings LIMIT 1').get() as any || {
    facility_name: 'グループホーム かがやきの家',
    capacity: 10
  };

  const residents = db.prepare(`
    SELECT r.*, u.unit_name
    FROM gh_residents r
    LEFT JOIN gh_units u ON r.unit_id = u.id
    WHERE r.status = 'active'
    ORDER BY r.room_number ASC
  `).all() as any[];

  const selectedResidentId = searchParams?.residentId || (residents[0]?.id || '');
  const selectedResident = residents.find(r => r.id === selectedResidentId) || residents[0];

  // 日付の配列生成
  const [yearStr, monthStr] = targetMonth.split('-');
  const year = parseInt(yearStr, 10);
  const month = parseInt(monthStr, 10);
  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();

  // 外泊データの取得
  const overnightStays = selectedResident ? db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM gh_overnight_stays
    WHERE resident_id = ?
      AND (
        (start_date <= ? AND (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= ?))
        OR (start_date LIKE ?)
      )
  `).all(
    selectedResident.id,
    `${targetMonth}-${String(daysInMonth).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    `${targetMonth}-01`,
    `${targetMonth}-%`
  ) as any[] : [];

  let stayDays = 0;
  let overnightDays = 0;
  let returnAdditionDays = 0;
  let breakfastCount = 0;
  let dinnerCount = 0;

  const rows = [];
  for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
    const dStr = String(d).padStart(2, '0');
    const dateStr = `${targetMonth}-${dStr}`;
    const dateObj = new Date(year, month - 1, d);
    const dayOfWeek = ['日', '月', '火', '水', '木', '金', '土'][dateObj.getDay()];

    // 外泊判定
    const matchingStay = overnightStays.find(s => {
      const start = s.start_date;
      const end = s.end_date || '9999-12-31';
      return dateStr >= start && dateStr <= end;
    });

    let serviceStatus = '1'; // 1: 在館（通常）
    let serviceLabel = '在館';
    let returnAddition = '-';
    let breakfast = '〇';
    let dinner = '〇';
    let remarks = '';

    if (matchingStay) {
      if (matchingStay.stay_type === 'hospital') {
        serviceStatus = '3';
        serviceLabel = '入院';
        remarks = '入院（外泊加算対象外）';
      } else {
        serviceStatus = '2';
        serviceLabel = '外泊';
        remarks = matchingStay.notes || '外泊（帰省）';
      }

      overnightDays++;

      if (matchingStay.return_addition_eligible === 1) {
        returnAddition = '〇';
        returnAdditionDays++;
      }

      if (matchingStay.deduct_breakfast === 1) {
        breakfast = '-';
      } else {
        breakfastCount++;
      }

      if (matchingStay.deduct_dinner === 1) {
        dinner = '-';
      } else {
        dinnerCount++;
      }
    } else {
      stayDays++;
      breakfastCount++;
      dinnerCount++;
    }

    rows.push({
      day: d,
      dayOfWeek,
      serviceStatus,
      serviceLabel,
      returnAddition,
      breakfast,
      dinner,
      remarks,
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 画面ヘッダー（印刷時非表示） */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 print:hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link
            href="/gh/billing"
            className="text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground flex items-center gap-1 font-medium"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            実費請求へ戻る
          </Link>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-950">
              共同生活援助 サービス提供実績記録票（別表2）
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-800">
              厚生労働省標準様式（別表2）準拠。在館・外泊日数、帰宅時加算、食事実績の法定記録原本
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {selectedResident && (
          <PrintButton
            label="実績記録票を印刷 / PDF"
            className="bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-bold h-9 text-xs shadow-sm"
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 絞り込みツールバー（印刷時非表示） */}
      <Card className="border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/20 shadow-xs print:hidden">
        <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-4">
          <form className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 items-end" method="GET">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700">対象月</label>
              <input
                type="month"
                name="month"
                defaultValue={targetMonth}
                className="flex h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-1 text-sm shadow-xs"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1 min-w-[200px]">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700">入居者</label>
              <select
                name="residentId"
                defaultValue={selectedResidentId}
                className="flex h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-1 text-sm shadow-xs"
              >
                {residents.map(r => (
                  <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                    {r.room_number}号室 {r.name} 様（区分{r.support_level}）
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <Button type="submit" className="h-9 px-4 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 font-bold text-white">
              表示更新
            </Button>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* A4印刷用紙コンテナ */}
      {selectedResident && (
        <div className="print-a4-sheet bg-white w-[210mm] min-h-[297mm] h-[297mm] p-[9mm_10mm_9mm_10mm] box-border text-black mx-auto shadow-2xl border border-gray-300 print:shadow-none print:border-none print:m-0 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden">
          {/* ヘッダー */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-center mb-3 print:mb-1.5">
              <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight print:text-[18px] print:mb-0.5">
                共同生活援助 サービス提供実績記録票（別表２）
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold print:text-[12px]">
                【 令和{year - 2018}年（{year}年） {month} 月分 】
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-3 print:mb-1.5 text-xs print:text-[10px] leading-tight">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p>受給者証番号： <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-sm print:text-[11px]">{selectedResident.recipient_number || '2810099999'}</span></p>
                <p className="text-sm print:text-[12px] border-b border-black inline-block min-w-[220px] pb-0.5 font-bold">
                  氏名： {selectedResident.name}　様（居室: {selectedResident.room_number}号室）
                </p>
                <p>障害支援区分： <span className="font-bold">区分{selectedResident.support_level}</span></p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right space-y-0.5">
                <p>契約支給量： <span className="font-bold text-sm print:text-[11px]">{daysInMonth}</span> 日/月</p>
                <p>事業所番号： <span className="font-mono font-semibold">2810099999</span></p>
                <p>事業者及びその事業所名： <span className="font-semibold">{settings.facility_name}</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* テーブル */}
            <table className="w-full border-collapse border border-black text-xs print:text-[10px] text-center table-fixed">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-gray-100 print:bg-gray-200 font-bold h-7 print:h-[6.5mm]">
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[7%]">日付</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[7%]">曜日</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[14%] text-[10px] print:text-[9px]">提供状況</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[12%] text-[10px] print:text-[9px]">帰宅時加算</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[10%] text-[10px] print:text-[9px]">朝食</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[10%] text-[10px] print:text-[9px]">夕食</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[28%]">備考（外泊先・帰省等）</th>
                  <th className="border border-black p-0.5 w-[12%] text-[10px] print:text-[9px]">確認印</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((row) => (
                  <tr
                    key={row.day}
                    className={`h-[6.2mm] print:h-[5.8mm] ${
                      row.dayOfWeek === '土' ? 'bg-blue-50/40 print:bg-transparent' : row.dayOfWeek === '日' ? 'bg-red-50/40 print:bg-transparent' : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 font-medium">{row.day}</td>
                    <td className={`border border-black p-0.5 font-medium ${row.dayOfWeek === '日' ? 'text-red-600 print:text-black' : row.dayOfWeek === '土' ? 'text-blue-600 print:text-black' : ''}`}>
                      {row.dayOfWeek}
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 font-bold">{row.serviceLabel}</td>
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 font-bold">{row.returnAddition}</td>
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 font-bold">{row.breakfast}</td>
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 font-bold">{row.dinner}</td>
                    <td className="border border-black p-0.5 text-left px-1.5 text-[10px] print:text-[9px] truncate">

                {/* 合計行 */}
                <tr className="bg-gray-100 print:bg-gray-200 font-bold h-7 print:h-[7mm]">

'use server';

import { getGhDb } from '../lib/db';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

export async function updateGhSettings(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getGhDb();
    const facilityName = (formData.get('facility_name') as string)?.trim() || 'グループホーム（共同生活援助）事業所';
    const capacity = parseInt(formData.get('capacity') as string, 10) || 10;
    const standardRent = parseInt(formData.get('standard_rent') as string, 10) || 35000;
    const specialBenefitDeduction = parseInt(formData.get('special_benefit_deduction') as string, 10) || 10000;
    const breakfastPrice = parseInt(formData.get('breakfast_price') as string, 10) || 300;
    const lunchPrice = parseInt(formData.get('lunch_price') as string, 10) || 450;
    const dinnerPrice = parseInt(formData.get('dinner_price') as string, 10) || 550;
    const utilitiesCost = parseInt(formData.get('utilities_cost') as string, 10) || 12000;
    const dailySuppliesCost = parseInt(formData.get('daily_supplies_cost') as string, 10) || 3000;

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE gh_settings
      SET facility_name = ?, capacity = ?, standard_rent = ?, special_benefit_deduction = ?,
          breakfast_price = ?, lunch_price = ?, dinner_price = ?, utilities_cost = ?,
          daily_supplies_cost = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      WHERE id = 'gh_default'
    `).run(
      facilityName, capacity, standardRent, specialBenefitDeduction,
      breakfastPrice, lunchPrice, dinnerPrice, utilitiesCost, dailySuppliesCost
    );

    revalidatePath('/gh/settings');
    revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
    revalidatePath('/gh/billing');
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('updateGhSettings error:', error);
  }
}

export async function addGhRoom(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getGhDb();
    const unitName = (formData.get('unit_name') as string)?.trim() || '本館 1号館';
    const roomNumber = (formData.get('room_number') as string)?.trim();
    const floor = parseInt(formData.get('floor') as string, 10) || 1;
    const capacity = parseInt(formData.get('capacity') as string, 10) || 1;
    const notes = (formData.get('notes') as string)?.trim() || '';

    if (!roomNumber) {
      return;
    }

    const id = `u_${Date.now()}`;
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO gh_units (id, unit_name, room_number, floor, capacity, is_occupied, notes)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
    `).run(id, unitName, roomNumber, floor, capacity, notes);

    revalidatePath('/gh/settings');
    revalidatePath('/settings');
    revalidatePath('/gh/residents');
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('addGhRoom error:', error);
  }
}

export async function deleteGhRoom(roomId: string) {
  try {
    if (!roomId) {
      return { success: false, error: '居室IDが指定されていません' };
    }
    const db = getGhDb();

    // 入居者の有無をチェック
    const occupied = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM gh_residents WHERE unit_id = ? AND status = 'active'").get(roomId) as any;
    if (occupied && occupied.cnt > 0) {
      return { 
        success: false, 
        error: '入居者が割り当てられているため削除できません。先に入居者を退去または別の居室へ移動してください。' 
      };
    }

    // 過去の退去済み履歴があれば unit_id を null にクリア
    db.prepare('UPDATE gh_residents SET unit_id = NULL WHERE unit_id = ?').run(roomId);

    // 居室本体の削除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM gh_units WHERE id = ?').run(roomId);

    try {
      revalidatePath('/gh/settings');
      revalidatePath('/settings');
      revalidatePath('/gh/residents');
      revalidatePath('/gh/dashboard');
    } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true };
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('deleteGhRoom error:', error);
    return { success: false, error: error?.message || '居室の削除に失敗しました' };
  }
}

'use server';

import { getHoudayDb } from '../lib/db';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

export async function saveHoudayAttendance(formData: FormData) {
  try {
    const db = getHoudayDb();
    const childId = formData.get('child_id') as string;
    const targetDate = (formData.get('target_date') as string)?.trim() || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const serviceType = (formData.get('service_type') as string) || 'school_day';
    const status = (formData.get('status') as string) || 'present';
    const arrivalTime = (formData.get('arrival_time') as string)?.trim() || '15:00';
    const departureTime = (formData.get('departure_time') as string)?.trim() || '17:30';
    const snackConsumed = formData.get('snack_consumed') === '1' ? 1 : 0;
    const pickupSchool = formData.get('pickup_school') === '1' ? 1 : 0;
    const dropoffHome = formData.get('dropoff_home') === '1' ? 1 : 0;
    const supportNote = (formData.get('support_note') as string)?.trim() || '';
    const staffName = (formData.get('staff_name') as string)?.trim() || '担当指導員';

    if (!childId) {
      return;
    }

    const id = `att_${childId}_${targetDate}`;
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO houday_attendance (
        id, child_id, target_date, service_type, status,
        arrival_time, departure_time, snack_consumed,
        pickup_school, dropoff_home, support_note, staff_name
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(child_id, target_date) DO UPDATE SET
        service_type = excluded.service_type,
        status = excluded.status,
        arrival_time = excluded.arrival_time,
        departure_time = excluded.departure_time,
        snack_consumed = excluded.snack_consumed,
        pickup_school = excluded.pickup_school,
        dropoff_home = excluded.dropoff_home,
        support_note = excluded.support_note,
        staff_name = excluded.staff_name
    `).run(
      id, childId, targetDate, serviceType, status,
      arrivalTime, departureTime, snackConsumed,
      pickupSchool, dropoffHome, supportNote, staffName
    );

    revalidatePath('/houday/attendance');
    revalidatePath('/houday/dashboard');
    revalidatePath('/houday/billing');
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('saveHoudayAttendance error:', error);
  }
}
